// src/components/support/CustomerSupportPortalModal.tsx
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import {
    Headset,
    MessageSquare,
    Wrench,
    CheckCircle2,
    Clock,
    ShieldCheck,
    Send,
    X,
    Activity,
    PhoneCall,
    Sparkles,
} from 'lucide-react';
import type { SupportTicket, UserSession } from '../../types/vyte.ts';
import { INITIAL_SUPPORT_TICKETS } from '../../data/mockData';

interface CustomerSupportPortalModalProps {
    isOpen: boolean;
    onClose: () => void;
    userSession: UserSession | null;
    onLogAction: (module: string, detail: string) => void;
    onNotify: (type: 'success' | 'error' | 'warning' | 'info', message: string) => void;
}

const SUPPORT_TICKETS_KEY = 'vyte_after_sales_tickets_v2';

export const CustomerSupportPortalModal: React.FC<CustomerSupportPortalModalProps> = ({
                                                                                          isOpen,
                                                                                          onClose,
                                                                                          userSession,
                                                                                          onLogAction,
                                                                                          onNotify,
                                                                                      }) => {
    const [tickets, setTickets] = useState<SupportTicket[]>(() => {
        try {
            const raw = localStorage.getItem(SUPPORT_TICKETS_KEY);
            return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : INITIAL_SUPPORT_TICKETS;
        } catch {
            return INITIAL_SUPPORT_TICKETS;
        }
    });

    const [subject, setSubject] = useState('');
    const [category, setCategory] = useState<SupportTicket['category']>('HARDWARE_SCANNER');
    const [priority, setPriority] = useState<SupportTicket['priority']>('HIGH');
    const [details, setDetails] = useState('');

    useEffect(() => {
        localStorage.setItem(SUPPORT_TICKETS_KEY, JSON.stringify(tickets));
    }, [tickets]);

    if (!isOpen) return null;

    const handleCreateTicket = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (!subject.trim()) return;

        const newTicket: SupportTicket = {
            ticketId: `SRV-2026-${Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000)}`,
            subject: subject.trim(),
            category,
            priority,
            status: 'OPEN_ASSIGNED',
            createdAt: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 16).replace('T', ' '),
            assignedEngineer:
                category === 'HARDWARE_SCANNER' || category === 'ON_SITE_MAINTENANCE'
                    ? 'Aiman Razak (Field Maintenance Engineer)'
                    : 'Jason Lim (Vyte Senior Support Specialist)',
            resolutionNote:
                details.trim() ||
                'Ticket logged. Assigned engineer will respond within 15 minutes via WhatsApp or Portal.',
        };

        setTickets((prev) => [newTicket, ...prev]);
        onLogAction(
            'After-Sales Support',
            `Submitted support ticket ${newTicket.ticketId} (${newTicket.subject}) [${newTicket.priority}].`
        );
        onNotify(
            'success',
            `Ticket ${newTicket.ticketId} dispatched to ${newTicket.assignedEngineer}.`
        );
        setSubject('');
        setDetails('');
    };

    const handleOpenWhatsAppSupport = () => {
        const msg = `Hi Vyte After-Sales Engineering Team! I need assistance for workspace "${
            userSession?.companyName || 'Vyte SME'
        }" (User: ${userSession?.userName || 'Owner'} / ID: ${
            userSession?.userID || 'M067'
        }). Issue: ${subject || 'Inventory & System Maintenance Support'}`;
        window.open(
            `https://wa.me/60123456789?text=${encodeURIComponent(msg)}`,
            '_blank',
            'noopener,noreferrer'
        );
        onLogAction(
            'After-Sales Support',
            'Initiated live WhatsApp chat with Vyte After-Sales Engineer.'
        );
    };

    return (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/65 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4 z-50 select-none">
            <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 max-w-3xl w-full p-6 shadow-2xl space-y-5 max-h-[92vh] overflow-y-auto">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-100 pb-4">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                        <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 text-[#2BB656] flex items-center justify-center">
                            <Headset className="w-5 h-5" />
                        </div>
                        <div>
                            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">
                                    Vyte After-Sales Care &amp; System Maintenance Portal
                                </h3>
                                <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 text-[10px] font-bold flex items-center gap-1">
                  <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-500 animate-ping" />
                  SLA 99.98% Online
                </span>
                            </div>
                            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                                Direct line to Vyte engineers for hardware calibration, data migration, and 24/7 SME operational support.
                            </p>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={onClose}
                        className="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100 cursor-pointer"
                    >
                        <X className="w-4 h-4" />
                    </button>
                </div>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-3 text-xs">
                    <div className="p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 text-white space-y-1">
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <span className="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-emerald-400 flex items-center gap-1">
                <Activity className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> System Health
              </span>
                            <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300 text-[10px] font-bold">
                All Systems Healthy
              </span>
                        </div>
                        <p className="font-bold text-xs pt-1">Inventory &amp; Order Engine: Active</p>
                        <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                            Next Auto-Backup &amp; Maintenance: Sun 03:00 AM (Zero Downtime)
                        </p>
                    </div>

                    <div className="p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 flex flex-col justify-between">
                        <div>
                            <p className="font-bold text-emerald-950 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                                <MessageSquare className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-600" /> 1-Click Live Engineer (WhatsApp)
                            </p>
                            <p className="text-[11px] text-emerald-800 mt-0.5">
                                Avg response time: <strong>&lt; 8 mins</strong> (Multi-language support)
                            </p>
                        </div>
                        <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={handleOpenWhatsAppSupport}
                            className="mt-2.5 w-full py-1.5 px-3 bg-[#25D366] hover:bg-[#1fb858] text-white rounded-lg font-bold text-xs flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 shadow-2xs cursor-pointer transition"
                        >
                            <PhoneCall className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Chat with Engineer Now
                        </button>
                    </div>

                    <div className="p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-blue-50 border border-blue-200 flex flex-col justify-between">
                        <div>
                            <p className="font-bold text-blue-950 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                                <Wrench className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-blue-600" /> Remote Diagnostics &amp; Sync
                            </p>
                            <p className="text-[11px] text-blue-800 mt-0.5">
                                Auto-verify catalog integrity, stock thresholds &amp; supplier webhook connectivity.
                            </p>
                        </div>
                        <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => {
                                onLogAction(
                                    'After-Sales Support',
                                    'Ran automated workspace health & inventory sync check.'
                                );
                                onNotify(
                                    'info',
                                    'Diagnostics complete: Catalog schema, stock thresholds & supplier connectors are 100% healthy.'
                                );
                            }}
                            className="mt-2.5 w-full py-1.5 px-3 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white rounded-lg font-bold text-xs flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 shadow-2xs cursor-pointer transition"
                        >
                            <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Run 1-Click Self-Check
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <form
                    onSubmit={handleCreateTicket}
                    className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 space-y-3 text-xs"
                >
                    <h4 className="font-bold text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                        <Wrench className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-600" /> Request System Maintenance or After-Sales Assistance
                    </h4>

                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
                        <div className="sm:col-span-1">
                            <label className="font-bold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Service Category</label>
                            <select
                                value={category}
                                onChange={(e) => setCategory(e.target.value as SupportTicket['category'])}
                                className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-white border border-slate-300 font-semibold text-slate-900"
                            >
                                <option value="HARDWARE_SCANNER">Barcode Scanner / POS Hardware</option>
                                <option value="INVENTORY_ENGINE">Auto-Restock / WhatsApp Bot Tuning</option>
                                <option value="DATA_MIGRATION">Data Import &amp; Catalog Setup</option>
                                <option value="ON_SITE_MAINTENANCE">Request On-Site Technician Visit</option>
                            </select>
                        </div>

                        <div className="sm:col-span-1">
                            <label className="font-bold text-slate-700 block mb-1">SLA Priority</label>
                            <select
                                value={priority}
                                onChange={(e) => setPriority(e.target.value as SupportTicket['priority'])}
                                className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-white border border-slate-300 font-semibold text-slate-900"
                            >
                                <option value="URGENT_SLA">URGENT (Response &lt; 15 mins)</option>
                                <option value="HIGH">HIGH (Same Business Day)</option>
                                <option value="NORMAL">NORMAL (Scheduled Maintenance)</option>
                            </select>
                        </div>

                        <div className="sm:col-span-1">
                            <label className="font-bold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Issue Summary *</label>
                            <input
                                type="text"
                                required
                                placeholder="e.g., Need help calibrating shelf scanner"
                                value={subject}
                                onChange={(e) => setSubject(e.target.value)}
                                className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-white border border-slate-300 text-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500"
                            />
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-stretch sm:items-center gap-2.5">
                        <input
                            type="text"
                            placeholder="Optional notes, preferred callback phone number, or remote support ID..."
                            value={details}
                            onChange={(e) => setDetails(e.target.value)}
                            className="flex-1 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-white border border-slate-300 text-slate-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500"
                        />
                        <button
                            type="submit"
                            className="px-4 py-2 bg-[#2BB656] hover:bg-emerald-600 text-white rounded-xl font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 shadow-xs cursor-pointer shrink-0"
                        >
                            <Send className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Submit Service Ticket
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </form>

                <div className="space-y-2">
                    <h4 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400">
                        Your Recent Maintenance &amp; Support Tickets ({tickets.length})
                    </h4>
                    <div className="space-y-2 max-h-52 overflow-y-auto pr-1">
                        {tickets.map((t) => (
                            <div
                                key={t.ticketId}
                                className="p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-2 text-xs"
                            >
                                <div className="space-y-0.5">
                                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="font-mono font-bold text-emerald-700 bg-emerald-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded">
                      {t.ticketId}
                    </span>
                                        <span className="font-bold text-slate-900">{t.subject}</span>
                                        <span className="text-[10px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 font-semibold">
                      {t.category.replace(/_/g, ' ')}
                    </span>
                                    </div>
                                    <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">
                                        Assigned: <strong>{t.assignedEngineer}</strong> â€¢ {t.resolutionNote}
                                    </p>
                                </div>

                                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
                                    {t.status === 'RESOLVED' ? (
                                        <span className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 font-bold text-[11px] inline-flex items-center gap-1">
                      <CheckCircle2 className="w-3 h-3" /> Resolved
                    </span>
                                    ) : (
                                        <button
                                            type="button"
                                            onClick={() =>
                                                setTickets((prev) =>
                                                    prev.map((x) =>
                                                        x.ticketId === t.ticketId ? { ...x, status: 'RESOLVED' } : x
                                                    )
                                                )
                                            }
                                            className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-amber-100 hover:bg-emerald-100 text-amber-900 hover:text-emerald-800 font-bold text-[11px] inline-flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer transition"
                                        >
                                            <Clock className="w-3 h-3" /> In Progress (Click to Close)
                                        </button>
                                    )}
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        ))}
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-3 border-t border-slate-100 text-[11px] text-slate-500">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <ShieldCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-600" />
            Included in your Vyte SME Subscription: Automated cloud backups &amp; 24/7 remote engineering support.
          </span>
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={onClose}
                        className="px-4 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 text-white font-bold text-xs cursor-pointer"
                    >
                        Close Portal
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};

========== FILE: C:\Users\User\WebstormProjects\Vyte\orbyteflow\src\modules\module1-restock\AddProductModal.tsx ==========

// src/modules/module1-restock/AddProductModal.tsx
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import type { CurrencyCode, IndustryProfile, NewProductForm } from '../../types/vyte.ts';
import { CURRENCY_SYMBOLS } from '../../types/vyte.ts';
import { VyteDropdown } from '../../components/common/VyteDropdown';

interface AddProductModalProps {
    isOpen: boolean;
    currency?: CurrencyCode;
    onClose: () => void;
    onSaveProduct: (form: NewProductForm) => void;
}

interface FormDraft {
    name: string; sku: string; category: string; industry: IndustryProfile; uom: string;
    stock: string; minThreshold: string; reorderQty: string; priceRM: string; unitCostRM: string;
    supplierName: string; supplierContact: string; batchNo: string; expiryDate: string;
    storageCondition: 'Ambient' | 'Chilled' | 'Frozen'; variantSpec: string; b2bMoq: string; serialOrWarranty: string;
}

const DRAFT_KEY = 'vyte_add_product_draft_v2';
const MY_PHONE_RE = /^(\+?60|0)1[0-9]-?[0-9]{7,8}$/;

export const AddProductModal: React.FC<AddProductModalProps> = ({ isOpen, currency = 'MYR', onClose, onSaveProduct }) => {
    const sym = CURRENCY_SYMBOLS[currency] || 'RM';
    const [draft, setDraft] = useState<FormDraft>(() => {
        try { const s = localStorage.getItem(DRAFT_KEY); if (s) return JSON.parse(s); } catch { /* ignore */ }
        return {
            name: '', sku: '', category: 'F&B Raw Ingredient', industry: 'FNB_PERISHABLE', uom: 'kg',
            stock: '', minThreshold: '', reorderQty: '', priceRM: '', unitCostRM: '', supplierName: '',
            supplierContact: '', batchNo: '', expiryDate: '', storageCondition: 'Chilled', variantSpec: '', b2bMoq: '', serialOrWarranty: '',
        };
    });
    const [errors, setErrors] = useState<Partial<Record<keyof FormDraft, string>>>({});

    useEffect(() => { localStorage.setItem(DRAFT_KEY, JSON.stringify(draft)); }, [draft]);
    if (!isOpen) return null;

    const validateField = (f: keyof FormDraft, val: string, d: FormDraft): string => {
        const v = val.trim();
        switch (f) {
            case 'name': return !v ? 'Product name is required.' : v.length < 3 ? 'Product name must be at least 3 characters.' : '';
            case 'sku': return v && !/^[A-Za-z0-9-_]{3,20}$/.test(v) ? 'SKU must be 3-20 alphanumeric characters (e.g., FNB-4886).' : '';
            case 'priceRM': return !v ? 'Selling price is required.' : v.startsWith('0') ? 'Selling price cannot start with 0.' : !/^[1-9]\d*(\.\d{1,2})?$/.test(v) ? 'Enter a valid price greater than 0 (e.g., 45.00).' : Number(v) > 1000000 ? `Selling price cannot exceed ${sym} 1,000,000.` : '';
            case 'stock': return !v ? 'Initial current stock is required.' : !/^\d+$/.test(v) ? 'Initial stock must be whole numbers (0-9).' : Number(v) > 999999 ? 'Initial stock cannot exceed 999,999.' : '';
            case 'minThreshold': return !v ? 'Alert threshold (min stock) is required.' : v.startsWith('0') || !/^[1-9]\d*$/.test(v) ? 'Alert threshold cannot start with 0 and must be at least 1.' : Number(v) > 99999 ? 'Alert threshold cannot exceed 99,999.' : '';
            case 'reorderQty': return !v ? 'Default reorder quantity is required.' : v.startsWith('0') || !/^[1-9]\d*$/.test(v) ? 'Reorder quantity cannot start with 0 and must be at least 1.' : '';
            case 'supplierName': return !v ? 'Supplier business name is required.' : v.length < 2 ? 'Supplier name must be at least 2 characters.' : '';
            case 'supplierContact': return !v ? 'Supplier WhatsApp number is required.' : !MY_PHONE_RE.test(v) ? 'Invalid WhatsApp number. Use MY format (e.g., +6012-3456789).' : '';
            case 'batchNo': return d.industry === 'FNB_PERISHABLE' && !v ? 'Batch number is required for F&B items.' : '';
            case 'expiryDate': return d.industry === 'FNB_PERISHABLE' && !v ? 'Expiry date is required for F&B items.' : '';
            case 'variantSpec': return d.industry === 'RETAIL_VARIANT' && !v ? 'Variant specification is required.' : '';
            case 'b2bMoq': return d.industry === 'B2B_RAW_MATERIAL' && !v ? 'B2B MOQ and lead time constraint is required.' : '';
            default: return '';
        }
    };

    const handleChange = (f: keyof FormDraft, val: string) => {
        const next = { ...draft, [f]: val };
        setDraft(next);
        setErrors((p) => ({ ...p, [f]: validateField(f, val, next) }));
    };

    const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        const keys: (keyof FormDraft)[] = ['name', 'sku', 'priceRM', 'stock', 'minThreshold', 'reorderQty', 'supplierName', 'supplierContact', 'batchNo', 'expiryDate', 'variantSpec', 'b2bMoq'];
        const nextErrs: Partial<Record<keyof FormDraft, string>> = {};
        keys.forEach((k) => { const err = validateField(k, draft[k], draft); if (err) nextErrs[k] = err; });
        setErrors(nextErrs);
        if (Object.keys(nextErrs).length > 0) return;

        const extraAttr = draft.industry === 'FNB_PERISHABLE'
            ? `Batch: ${draft.batchNo.trim()} | Exp: ${draft.expiryDate.trim()} | Storage: ${draft.storageCondition}`
            : draft.industry === 'RETAIL_VARIANT' ? draft.variantSpec.trim() : draft.b2bMoq.trim();

        onSaveProduct({
            name: draft.name.trim(), sku: draft.sku.trim(), category: draft.category, industry: draft.industry, uom: draft.uom,
            stock: Number(draft.stock), minThreshold: Number(draft.minThreshold), reorderQty: Number(draft.reorderQty),
            priceRM: Number(draft.priceRM), supplierName: draft.supplierName.trim(), supplierContact: draft.supplierContact.trim(), extraAttr,
        });
        onClose();
    };

    const inputCls = (f: keyof FormDraft) =>
        `w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white text-slate-900 placeholder-slate-400 border rounded-xl text-xs font-medium focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 transition ${
            errors[f] ? 'border-rose-500 focus:border-rose-500 focus:ring-rose-500/20 bg-rose-50/20' : 'border-slate-300 focus:border-[#2BB656] focus:ring-[#2BB656]/20'
        }`;

    const errEl = (f: keyof FormDraft) => errors[f] ? <p className="text-[11px] text-rose-600 font-semibold mt-1 flex items-center gap-1"><AlertCircle className="w-3 h-3 shrink-0" /> {errors[f]}</p> : null;

    return (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4 z-50 overflow-y-auto">
            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} noValidate className="bg-white text-slate-900 rounded-2xl max-w-lg w-full p-6 space-y-4 shadow-2xl border border-slate-200 my-8">
                <div className="border-b border-slate-100 pb-3"><h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">Add New Product</h3></div>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-xs">
                    <div className="col-span-2">
                        <label className="font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Industry Profile</label>
                        <VyteDropdown<IndustryProfile>
                            variant="light" value={draft.industry}
                            onChange={(ind) => {
                                setDraft({
                                    ...draft, industry: ind,
                                    category: ind === 'FNB_PERISHABLE' ? 'F&B Raw Ingredient' : ind === 'RETAIL_VARIANT' ? 'Apparel & Accessories' : 'Packaging & Raw Material',
                                    uom: ind === 'FNB_PERISHABLE' ? 'kg' : ind === 'RETAIL_VARIANT' ? 'pcs' : 'box',
                                });
                                setErrors({});
                            }}
                            options={[
                                { label: 'F&B / Cafe / Grocery (Batch & Expiry)', value: 'FNB_PERISHABLE' },
                                { label: 'Retail & Boutique (Sizes & Colors)', value: 'RETAIL_VARIANT' },
                                { label: 'B2B Manufacturing (MOQ & Lead Time)', value: 'B2B_RAW_MATERIAL' },
                            ]}
                        />
                    </div>

                    <div className="col-span-2 sm:col-span-1">
                        <label className="font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Product Name *</label>
                        <input type="text" placeholder="e.g., Premium Matcha Powder" value={draft.name} onChange={(e) => handleChange('name', e.target.value)} className={inputCls('name')} />

                        {errEl('priceRM')}

                        <label className="font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Initial Current Stock *</label>

                        {errEl('minThreshold')}
                    </div>

                    <div className="col-span-2">
                        <label className="font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Default Reorder Quantity *</label>
                        <input type="text" placeholder="e.g., 30" value={draft.reorderQty} onChange={(e) => handleChange('reorderQty', e.target.value)} className={inputCls('reorderQty')} />
                        {errEl('reorderQty')}
                    </div>

                    <div>
                        <label className="font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Supplier Business Name *</label>
                        <input type="text" placeholder="e.g., Uji Wholesale MY" value={draft.supplierName} onChange={(e) => handleChange('supplierName', e.target.value)} className={inputCls('supplierName')} />
                        {errEl('supplierName')}
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <label className="font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Supplier WhatsApp Number *</label>
                        <input type="tel" placeholder="e.g., +6012-3456789" value={draft.supplierContact} onChange={(e) => handleChange('supplierContact', e.target.value)} className={inputCls('supplierContact')} />
                        {errEl('supplierContact')}
                    </div>

                    {draft.industry === 'FNB_PERISHABLE' && (
                        <>
                            <div>
                                <label className="font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Batch Number *</label>
                                <input type="text" placeholder="e.g., B-2026-10" value={draft.batchNo} onChange={(e) => handleChange('batchNo', e.target.value)} className={inputCls('batchNo')} />
                                {errEl('batchNo')}
                            </div>
                            <div>
                                <label className="font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Expiry Date *</label>
                                <input type="date" value={draft.expiryDate} onChange={(e) => handleChange('expiryDate', e.target.value)} className={inputCls('expiryDate')} />
                                {errEl('expiryDate')}
                            </div>
                            <div className="col-span-2">
                                <label className="font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Storage Condition</label>
                                <VyteDropdown<'Ambient' | 'Chilled' | 'Frozen'>
                                    variant="light" value={draft.storageCondition} onChange={(v) => setDraft({ ...draft, storageCondition: v })}
                                    options={[
                                        { label: 'Ambient / Room Temperature', value: 'Ambient' },
                                        { label: 'Chilled (2\u00B0C - 4\u00B0C)', value: 'Chilled' },
                                        { label: 'Frozen (-18\u00B0C)', value: 'Frozen' },
                                    ]}
                                />
                            </div>
                        </>
                    )}

                    {draft.industry === 'RETAIL_VARIANT' && (
                        <>
                            <div className="col-span-2">
                                <label className="font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Variant Specification *</label>
                                <input type="text" placeholder="e.g., Size: L | Color: Matte Black" value={draft.variantSpec} onChange={(e) => handleChange('variantSpec', e.target.value)} className={inputCls('variantSpec')} />
                                {errEl('variantSpec')}
                            </div>
                            <div className="col-span-2">
                                <label className="font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Serial / Warranty Info</label>
                                <input type="text" placeholder="e.g., 1-Year Local Manufacturer Warranty" value={draft.serialOrWarranty} onChange={(e) => setDraft({ ...draft, serialOrWarranty: e.target.value })} className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white border border-slate-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-medium focus:outline-none focus:border-[#2BB656]" />
                            </div>
                        </>
                    )}

                    {draft.industry === 'B2B_RAW_MATERIAL' && (
                        <div className="col-span-2">
                            <label className="font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">B2B Order Constraints *</label>
                            <input type="text" placeholder="e.g., MOQ: 20 Boxes | Lead Time: 3 Days" value={draft.b2bMoq} onChange={(e) => handleChange('b2bMoq', e.target.value)} className={inputCls('b2bMoq')} />
                            {errEl('b2bMoq')}
                        </div>
                    )}
                </div>

                <div className="flex justify-end gap-2.5 pt-3 border-t border-slate-100">
                    <button type="button" onClick={onClose} className="px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-200 bg-slate-100 rounded-xl transition cursor-pointer">Cancel</button>
                    <button type="submit" className="px-4 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-white bg-[#2BB656] hover:bg-emerald-600 rounded-xl shadow-xs transition cursor-pointer">Save Product</button>
                </div>
            </form>
        </div>
    );
};

// src/modules/module1-restock/RestockModule.tsx
import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect, useMemo } from 'react';
import {
    Plus,
    Send,
    CheckCircle2,
    Clock,
    AlertTriangle,
    ShoppingBag,
    Lock,
    AlertCircle,
    Loader2,
    FileSpreadsheet,
    Camera,
    X,
    Database,
    Scale,
    Sparkles,
    Award,
    MessageSquarePlus,
    ShieldAlert,
    Check,
    Trash2,
    History,
    Archive,
} from 'lucide-react';
import type {
    CurrencyCode,
    Product,
    SupplierQuotationBid,
    UserRole,
} from '../../types/vyte.ts';
import { CURRENCY_SYMBOLS } from '../../types/vyte.ts';
import { INITIAL_SUPPLIER_QUOTATIONS } from '../../data/mockData';

interface RestockModuleProps {
    products: Product[];
    role: UserRole;
    currency?: CurrencyCode;
    exchangeRate?: number;
    onApproveAndContactSupplier: (
        productId: string,
        customReorderQty?: number,
        awardedBid?: SupplierQuotationBid
    ) => Promise<void> | void;
    onConfirmSupplierDelivery: (productId: string) => Promise<void> | void;
    onRecordStockUsage: (productId: string, usedQty: number) => void;
    onOpenAddModal: () => void;
    onBatchImportProducts?: (imported: Product[]) => void;
    onOpenMigratorModal?: () => void;
    onRemoveProduct?: (productId: string) => void;
}

const USAGE_KEY = 'vyte_restock_usage_inputs_v2';
const REORDER_KEY = 'vyte_restock_reorder_inputs_v2';
const QUOTATIONS_KEY = 'vyte_supplier_quotations_v1';

const sanitizeInt = (raw: string, maxDigits = 5) =>
    raw.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '').replace(/^0+/, '').slice(0, maxDigits);

export const RestockModule: React.FC<RestockModuleProps> = ({
                                                                products,
                                                                role,
                                                                currency = 'MYR',
                                                                exchangeRate = 1.0,
                                                                onApproveAndContactSupplier,
                                                                onConfirmSupplierDelivery,
                                                                onRecordStockUsage,
                                                                onOpenAddModal,
                                                                onBatchImportProducts,
                                                                onOpenMigratorModal,
                                                                onRemoveProduct,
                                                            }) => {
    const sym = CURRENCY_SYMBOLS[currency] || 'RM';
    const canApprove = role === 'SME_OWNER' || role === 'MANAGER';

    const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState<'catalog' | 'quotations'>('catalog');
    const [showArchivedOnly, setShowArchivedOnly] = useState<boolean>(false);
    const [selectedRfqProductId, setSelectedRfqProductId] = useState<string>('ALL');
    const [selectedQuotationNos, setSelectedQuotationNos] = useState<string[]>([]);

    const [quotations, setQuotations] = useState<SupplierQuotationBid[]>(() => {
        try {
            const raw = localStorage.getItem(QUOTATIONS_KEY);
            return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : INITIAL_SUPPLIER_QUOTATIONS;
        } catch {
            return INITIAL_SUPPLIER_QUOTATIONS;
        }
    });

    useEffect(() => {
        localStorage.setItem(QUOTATIONS_KEY, JSON.stringify(quotations));
    }, [quotations]);

    const [pendingDecommissionProduct, setPendingDecommissionProduct] =
        useState<Product | null>(null);

    const [reviewModalBid, setReviewModalBid] =
        useState<SupplierQuotationBid | null>(null);
    const [sampleStatusInput, setSampleStatusInput] =
        useState<SupplierQuotationBid['sampleTestStatus']>('PASSED');
    const [defectRateInput, setDefectRateInput] = useState<string>('0.5');
    const [staffCommentInput, setStaffCommentInput] = useState<string>('');
    const [pastExpInput, setPastExpInput] = useState<string>('');
    const [staffNameInput, setStaffNameInput] = useState<string>(
        'Amirul Hakim (Warehouse QC)'
    );

    const [showNewBidModal, setShowNewBidModal] = useState(false);
    const [newBidProductId, setNewBidProductId] = useState<string>(
        products[0]?.id || 'P1'
    );
    const [newBidSupplierName, setNewBidSupplierName] = useState('');
    const [newBidSupplierCode, setNewBidSupplierCode] = useState('108200');
    const [newBidAddress, setNewBidAddress] = useState('');
    const [newBidPhone, setNewBidPhone] = useState('+6012-5566778');
    const [newBidUnitPrice, setNewBidUnitPrice] = useState('');
    const [newBidDefectPct, setNewBidDefectPct] = useState('1.0');
    const [newBidSampleStatus, setNewBidSampleStatus] =
        useState<SupplierQuotationBid['sampleTestStatus']>('PASSED');
    const [newBidComment, setNewBidComment] = useState('');

    const [usageInputs, setUsageInputs] = useState<Record<string, string>>(() => {
        try {
            const s = localStorage.getItem(USAGE_KEY);
            return s ? JSON.parse(s) : {};
        } catch {
            return {};
        }
    });
    const [usageErrors, setUsageErrors] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});

    const [reorderInputs, setReorderInputs] = useState<Record<string, string>>(() => {
        try {
            const s = localStorage.getItem(REORDER_KEY);
            return s ? JSON.parse(s) : {};
        } catch {
            return {};
        }
    });
    const [reorderErrors, setReorderErrors] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});

    const [pendingSaleConfirm, setPendingSaleConfirm] = useState<{
        item: Product;
        qty: number;
    } | null>(null);
    const [approvingIds, setApprovingIds] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});
    const [confirmingIds, setConfirmingIds] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});

    const excelInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
    const photoInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

    useEffect(() => {
        localStorage.setItem(USAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(usageInputs));
    }, [usageInputs]);
    useEffect(() => {
        localStorage.setItem(REORDER_KEY, JSON.stringify(reorderInputs));
    }, [reorderInputs]);

    const computeAiEvaluation = (
        unitPriceRM: number,
        lowestPriceForProductRM: number,
        defectRatePct: number,
        sampleStatus: SupplierQuotationBid['sampleTestStatus'],
        supplierName: string
    ): {
        score: number;
        rec: SupplierQuotationBid['aiRecommendation'];
        note: string;
    } => {
        const priceRatioPenalty =
            lowestPriceForProductRM > 0
                ? Math.max(
                    0,
                    ((unitPriceRM - lowestPriceForProductRM) / lowestPriceForProductRM) * 60
                )
                : 0;
        const defectPenalty = defectRatePct * 3.8;
        const sampleAdjustment =
            sampleStatus === 'PASSED'
                ? 12
                : sampleStatus === 'WARNING'
                    ? -12
                    : sampleStatus === 'FAILED'
                        ? -32
                        : 0;
        const rawScore = Math.round(
            90 - priceRatioPenalty - defectPenalty + sampleAdjustment
        );
        const score = Math.max(15, Math.min(99, rawScore));

        const isLowestPrice = Math.abs(unitPriceRM - lowestPriceForProductRM) < 0.01;

        if (defectRatePct >= 5 || sampleStatus === 'FAILED') {
            return {
                score,
                rec: isLowestPrice ? 'LOWEST_PRICE_HIGH_RISK' : 'AVOID_DEFECT_RISK',
                note: isLowestPrice
                    ? `Lowest price bid, but flagged for ${defectRatePct}% past defect rate and ${sampleStatus.toLowerCase()} sample test. High risk of batch loss.`
                    : `High defect risk (${defectRatePct}% historical defect rate, sample ${sampleStatus.toLowerCase()}). Not recommended.`,
            };
        }
        if (score >= 88) {
            return {
                score,
                rec: 'RECOMMENDED_BEST_VALUE',
                note: `AI Recommended (${score}/100): ${supplierName} combines low defect history (${defectRatePct}%) and passed warehouse sample testing.`,
            };
        }
        return {
            score,
            rec: 'ACCEPTABLE_BACKUP',
            note: `Acceptable secondary supplier (${score}/100) with ${defectRatePct}% past defect rate.`,
        };
    };

    const filteredQuotations = useMemo(() => {
        const list =
            selectedRfqProductId === 'ALL'
                ? quotations
                : quotations.filter((q) => q.productId === selectedRfqProductId);
        return [...list].sort((a, b) => {
            if (a.productId !== b.productId) return a.productId.localeCompare(b.productId);
            return a.totalNetValueRM - b.totalNetValueRM;
        });
    }, [quotations, selectedRfqProductId]);

    const lowestNetValueByProduct = useMemo(() => {
        const map: Record<string, number> = {};
        quotations.forEach((q) => {
            if (map[q.productId] === undefined || q.totalNetValueRM < map[q.productId]) {
                map[q.productId] = q.totalNetValueRM;
            }
        });
        return map;
    }, [quotations]);

    const handleAwardQuotation = async (bid: SupplierQuotationBid) => {
        if (!canApprove) return;
        setQuotations((prev) =>
            prev.map((q) => {
                if (q.productId !== bid.productId) return q;
                if (q.quotationNo === bid.quotationNo) return { ...q, status: 'Awarded' };
                return { ...q, status: 'Completed' };
            })
        );
        await onApproveAndContactSupplier(bid.productId, bid.orderQty, bid);
    };

    const handleSaveSampleReview = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (!reviewModalBid) return;
        const parsedDefect = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Number(defectRateInput) || 0));
        const lowestUnit = Math.min(
            ...quotations
                .filter((q) => q.productId === reviewModalBid.productId)
                .map((q) => q.unitPriceRM)
        );
        const evalRes = computeAiEvaluation(
            reviewModalBid.unitPriceRM,
            lowestUnit,
            parsedDefect,
            sampleStatusInput,
            reviewModalBid.supplierName
        );

        setQuotations((prev) =>
            prev.map((q) =>
                q.quotationNo === reviewModalBid.quotationNo
                    ? {
                        ...q,
                        pastDefectRatePct: parsedDefect,
                        sampleTestStatus: sampleStatusInput,
                        staffSampleComment:
                            staffCommentInput.trim() || q.staffSampleComment,
                        pastExperienceNote: pastExpInput.trim() || q.pastExperienceNote,
                        testedByStaff: staffNameInput.trim() || q.testedByStaff,
                        aiQualityScore: evalRes.score,
                        aiRecommendation: evalRes.rec,
                        aiAnalysisNote: evalRes.note,
                    }
                    : q
            )
        );
        setReviewModalBid(null);
    };

    const handleAddSupplierBid = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        const prod = products.find((p) => p.id === newBidProductId) || products[0];
        if (!prod || !newBidSupplierName.trim() || !newBidUnitPrice) return;

        const unitPriceRM = Number(
            (Number(newBidUnitPrice) / exchangeRate).toFixed(2)
        );
        const orderQty = prod.reorderQty || 50;
        const totalNetValueRM = Number((unitPriceRM * orderQty).toFixed(2));
        const defectPct = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Number(newBidDefectPct) || 0));
        const nextQuotationNo = String(8000000000 + quotations.length + 10);

        const existingForProd = quotations.filter((q) => q.productId === prod.id);
        const lowestUnit = Math.min(
            unitPriceRM,
            ...existingForProd.map((q) => q.unitPriceRM)
        );
        const evalRes = computeAiEvaluation(
            unitPriceRM,
            lowestUnit,
            defectPct,
            newBidSampleStatus,
            newBidSupplierName.trim()
        );

        const createdBid: SupplierQuotationBid = {
            quotationNo: nextQuotationNo,
            rfqNo: `700000000${prod.id.replace(/\D/g, '') || '1'}`,
            productId: prod.id,
            sku: prod.sku,
            productName: prod.name,
            orderQty,
            uom: prod.uom,
            supplierCode: newBidSupplierCode.trim() || '108200',
            supplierName: newBidSupplierName.trim(),
            supplierContact: newBidPhone.trim() || '+6012-5566778',
            address:
                newBidAddress.trim() ||
                '25 Jalan Perusahaan, Shah Alam 40000, Malaysia',
            status: 'Submitted',
            unitPriceRM,
            totalNetValueRM,
            quotationDate: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
            deliveryLeadDays: 2,
            fullyQuotedItems: '1/1',
            bestPricedItems: unitPriceRM <= lowestUnit ? '1/1' : '0/1',
            pastOrdersCount: 1,
            pastDefectRatePct: defectPct,
            pastExperienceNote:
                defectPct > 5
                    ? `Logged ${defectPct}% defect rate in prior delivery.`
                    : `Verified ${defectPct}% defect rate.`,
            sampleTestStatus: newBidSampleStatus,
            staffSampleComment:
                newBidComment.trim() || 'Sample inspected by warehouse staff.',
            testedByStaff: 'Amirul Hakim (Warehouse QC)',
            aiQualityScore: evalRes.score,
            aiRecommendation: evalRes.rec,
            aiAnalysisNote: evalRes.note,
        };

        setQuotations((prev) => [createdBid, ...prev]);
        setNewBidSupplierName('');
        setNewBidUnitPrice('');
        setNewBidComment('');
        setShowNewBidModal(false);
    };

    const handleSpreadsheetImport = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
        const file = e.target.files?.[0];
        if (!file || !onBatchImportProducts) return;
        const reader = new FileReader();
        reader.onload = () => {
            const lines = String(reader.result || '')
                .split(/\r?\n/)
                .map((l) => l.trim())
                .filter(Boolean);
            const imported: Product[] = [];
            lines.forEach((line, idx) => {
                if (idx === 0 && line.toLowerCase().includes('sku')) return;
                const cols = line.split(',').map((c) => c.trim().replace(/^"|"$/g, ''));
                if (cols.length >= 4) {
                    const stockNum = Number(cols[2]) || 8;
                    const minNum = Number(cols[3]) || 15;
                    imported.push({
                        id: `P-XLS-${Date.now()}-${idx}`,
                        sku: cols[0] || `XLS-${1000 + idx}`,
                        name: cols[1] || `Imported SKU #${idx + 1}`,
                        category: cols[4] || 'F&B Raw Ingredient',
                        industry: 'FNB_PERISHABLE',
                        uom: cols[5] || 'kg',
                        stock: stockNum,
                        minThreshold: minNum,
                        reorderQty: Number(cols[6]) || 30,
                        priceRM: Number(cols[7]) || 52.0,
                        unitCostRM: 31.0,
                        dailyDepletionRate: 3.5,
                        leadTimeDays: 2,
                        moq: 10,
                        supplierName: cols[8] || 'Verified Supplier MY',
                        supplierContact: cols[9] || '+6012-8899221',
                        performance: 'Good',
                        visibility: true,
                        batchNo: `B-2026-X${idx}`,
                        expiryDate: '2026-11-20',
                        restockStatus:
                            stockNum <= minNum ? 'TRIGGERED_PENDING_APPROVAL' : 'HEALTHY',
                    });
                }
            });
            if (imported.length > 0) onBatchImportProducts(imported);
            e.target.value = '';
        };
        reader.readAsText(file);
    };

    const handleShelfPhotoUpload = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
        const file = e.target.files?.[0];
        if (!file || !onBatchImportProducts) return;
        const reader = new FileReader();
        reader.onload = () => {
            onBatchImportProducts([
                {
                    id: `P-IMG-${Date.now()}`,
                    sku: `IMG-${Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000)}`,
                    name: `Scanned Item (${file.name.slice(0, 14)})`,
                    category: 'F&B Raw Ingredient',
                    industry: 'FNB_PERISHABLE',
                    uom: 'pack',
                    stock: 5,
                    minThreshold: 12,
                    reorderQty: 30,
                    priceRM: 38.0,
                    unitCostRM: 22.0,
                    dailyDepletionRate: 3.0,
                    leadTimeDays: 2,
                    moq: 10,
                    supplierName: 'Klang Valley Roasters Sdn Bhd',
                    supplierContact: '+6012-3456789',
                    performance: 'Good',
                    visibility: true,
                    batchNo: 'B-PHOTO-26',
                    expiryDate: '2026-11-10',
                    photoUrl: String(reader.result),
                    restockStatus: 'TRIGGERED_PENDING_APPROVAL',
                },
            ]);
            e.target.value = '';
        };
        reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    };

    const handleRequestSaleConfirmation = (item: Product) => {
        if (item.stock <= 0) return;
        const rawStr = usageInputs[item.id] ?? '3';
        const qty = Number(rawStr);
        if (!rawStr || isNaN(qty) || qty < 1) {
            return setUsageErrors((p) => ({ ...p, [item.id]: 'Enter at least 1.' }));
        }
        if (qty > item.stock) {
            return setUsageErrors((p) => ({
                ...p,
                [item.id]: `Max available is ${item.stock} ${item.uom}.`,
            }));
        }
        setUsageErrors((p) => ({ ...p, [item.id]: '' }));
        setPendingSaleConfirm({ item, qty });
    };

    const handleApprovePO = async (item: Product) => {
        const rawReorder = reorderInputs[item.id] ?? String(item.reorderQty);
        const qty = Number(rawReorder);
        const minMoq = item.moq ?? 5;
        if (!rawReorder || isNaN(qty) || qty < minMoq) {
            return setReorderErrors((p) => ({
                ...p,
                [item.id]: `Min supplier order is ${minMoq} ${item.uom}.`,
            }));
        }
        if (qty > 10000) {
            return setReorderErrors((p) => ({
                ...p,
                [item.id]: 'Max single PO limit is 10,000 units.',
            }));
        }

        setReorderErrors((p) => ({ ...p, [item.id]: '' }));
        setApprovingIds((p) => ({ ...p, [item.id]: true }));
        try {
            await onApproveAndContactSupplier(item.id, qty);
        } finally {
            setApprovingIds((p) => ({ ...p, [item.id]: false }));
        }
    };

    const handleConfirmDeliveryClick = async (item: Product) => {
        setConfirmingIds((p) => ({ ...p, [item.id]: true }));
        try {
            await onConfirmSupplierDelivery(item.id);
        } finally {
            setConfirmingIds((p) => ({ ...p, [item.id]: false }));
        }
    };

    const activeProducts = useMemo(
        () =>
            products.filter(
                (p) => !p.isRemoved && p.visibility !== false && !(p as any).isArchived
            ),
        [products]
    );
    const archivedProducts = useMemo(
        () =>
            products.filter(
                (p) => p.isRemoved || p.visibility === false || (p as any).isArchived
            ),
        [products]
    );

    const displayedProducts = useMemo(() => {
        const base = showArchivedOnly ? archivedProducts : activeProducts;
        return [...base].sort((a, b) => {
            const rank = (s: Product['restockStatus']) =>
                s === 'TRIGGERED_PENDING_APPROVAL'
                    ? 0
                    : s === 'SUPPLIER_CONTACTED'
                        ? 1
                        : s === 'CONFIRMED'
                            ? 2
                            : 3;
            return rank(a.restockStatus) - rank(b.restockStatus);
        });
    }, [showArchivedOnly, activeProducts, archivedProducts]);

    return (
        <div className="space-y-4">
            <input
                ref={excelInputRef}
                type="file"
                accept=".csv,.xlsx,.xls"
                onChange={handleSpreadsheetImport}
                className="hidden"
            />
            <input
                ref={photoInputRef}
                type="file"
                accept="image/*"
                onChange={handleShelfPhotoUpload}
                className="hidden"
            />

            <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-5 py-3.5 shadow-xs flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
                    <div className="inline-flex items-center bg-slate-100 p-1 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 text-xs font-semibold">
                        <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => {
                                setViewMode('catalog');
                                setShowArchivedOnly(false);
                            }}
                            className={`px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition cursor-pointer ${
                                viewMode === 'catalog' && !showArchivedOnly
                                    ? 'bg-slate-900 text-white font-bold shadow-2xs'
                                    : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900'
                            }`}
                        >
                            Active Catalog ({activeProducts.length})
                        </button>
                        <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => {
                                setViewMode('catalog');
                                setShowArchivedOnly(true);
                            }}
                            className={`px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1.5 ${
                                viewMode === 'catalog' && showArchivedOnly
                                    ? 'bg-amber-600 text-white font-bold shadow-2xs'
                                    : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900'
                            }`}
                        >
                            <History className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                            <span>Decommissioned History ({archivedProducts.length})</span>
                        </button>
                        <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => setViewMode('quotations')}
                            className={`px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1.5 ${
                                viewMode === 'quotations'
                                    ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white font-bold shadow-2xs'
                                    : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900'
                            }`}
                        >
                            <Scale className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                            <span>Supplier Quotations &amp; AI Evaluation ({quotations.length})</span>
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                    {viewMode === 'quotations' ? (
                        <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => setShowNewBidModal(true)}
                            className="px-3.5 py-1.5 bg-[#2BB656] hover:bg-emerald-600 text-white rounded-lg text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-xs transition cursor-pointer"
                        >
                            <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Add Supplier Quotation
                        </button>
                    ) : (
                        <>
                            {onOpenMigratorModal && (
                                <button
                                    type="button"
                                    onClick={onOpenMigratorModal}
                                    className="px-3.5 py-1.5 bg-emerald-50 hover:bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border border-emerald-200 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition cursor-pointer"
                                >
                                    <Database className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#2BB656]" /> Import System Data
                                </button>
                            )}
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => photoInputRef.current?.click()}
                                className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-purple-50 hover:bg-purple-100 text-purple-800 border border-purple-200 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition cursor-pointer"
                            >
                                <Camera className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-purple-600" /> Scan Shelf Photo
                            </button>
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => excelInputRef.current?.click()}
                                className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-teal-50 hover:bg-teal-100 text-teal-800 border border-teal-200 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition cursor-pointer"
                            >
                                <FileSpreadsheet className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-teal-600" /> Import CSV
                            </button>
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={onOpenAddModal}
                                className="px-3.5 py-1.5 bg-[#2BB656] hover:bg-emerald-600 text-white rounded-lg text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-xs transition cursor-pointer shrink-0"
                            >
                                <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Add Product
                            </button>
                        </>
                    )}
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* VIEW 1: SUPPLIER QUOTATIONS & AI BID EVALUATION */}
            {viewMode === 'quotations' && (
                <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs overflow-hidden space-y-4">
                    <div className="px-5 py-4 border-b border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/70 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
                        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
                            <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">
                                Quotations ({filteredQuotations.length})
                            </h3>
                            <select
                                value={selectedRfqProductId}
                                onChange={(e) => setSelectedRfqProductId(e.target.value)}
                                className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-white border border-slate-300 text-xs font-bold text-slate-800"
                            >
                                <option value="ALL">
                                    All RFQ Products ({quotations.length} Bids)
                                </option>
                                {activeProducts.map((p) => (
                                    <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>
                                        {p.name} ({p.sku}) -{' '}
                                        {quotations.filter((q) => q.productId === p.id).length} Quotes
                                    </option>
                                ))}
                            </select>
                            <span className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                Evaluates net quotation price alongside past defect memory and warehouse sample tests.
              </span>
                        </div>

                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                disabled={selectedQuotationNos.length === 0 || !canApprove}
                                onClick={() => {
                                    const targetBid = quotations.find(
                                        (q) => q.quotationNo === selectedQuotationNos[0]
                                    );
                                    if (targetBid) void handleAwardQuotation(targetBid);
                                }}
                                className="px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:bg-slate-200 disabled:text-slate-400 text-white font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition cursor-pointer"
                            >
                                <Award className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Award Selected Quote
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                        <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse text-xs">
                            <thead>
                            <tr className="border-b border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-600">
                                <th className="py-3 px-3 w-10 text-center">Select</th>
                                <th className="py-3 px-3">Supplier Quotation</th>
                                <th className="py-3 px-3">Supplier</th>
                                <th className="py-3 px-3">Address</th>
                                <th className="py-3 px-3">Status</th>
                                <th className="py-3 px-3">Total Quotation Net Value</th>
                                <th className="py-3 px-3">Quotation Date</th>
                                <th className="py-3 px-3 text-center">Fully Quoted Items</th>
                                <th className="py-3 px-3 text-center">Best-Priced Items</th>
                                <th className="py-3 px-3">AI Quality Score &amp; Past Defect Memory</th>
                                <th className="py-3 px-3">Warehouse Sample Test Comment</th>
                                <th className="py-3 px-4 text-right">Bid Action</th>
                            </tr>
                            </thead>
                            <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-200/80">
                            {filteredQuotations.map((bid) => {
                                const isLowestNet =
                                    lowestNetValueByProduct[bid.productId] === bid.totalNetValueRM;
                                const isSelected = selectedQuotationNos.includes(bid.quotationNo);

                                return (
                                    <tr
                                        key={bid.quotationNo}
                                        className={`transition ${
                                            bid.status === 'Awarded'
                                                ? 'bg-emerald-50/40'
                                                : bid.aiRecommendation === 'RECOMMENDED_BEST_VALUE'
                                                    ? 'bg-blue-50/20 hover:bg-blue-50/40'
                                                    : 'hover:bg-slate-50'
                                        }`}
                                    >
                                        <td className="py-3.5 px-3 text-center">
                                            <input
                                                type="checkbox"
                                                checked={isSelected}
                                                onChange={() =>
                                                    setSelectedQuotationNos((prev) =>
                                                        prev.includes(bid.quotationNo)
                                                            ? prev.filter((id) => id !== bid.quotationNo)
                                                            : [bid.quotationNo]
                                                    )
                                                }
                                                className="w-4 h-4 accent-blue-600 cursor-pointer"
                                            />
                                        </td>
                                        <td className="py-3.5 px-4 whitespace-nowrap">
                                            <p className="font-mono font-extrabold text-blue-700">
                                                {bid.quotationNo}
                                            </p>
                                            <p className="font-bold text-slate-900 text-xs mt-0.5">
                                                {bid.productName}
                                            </p>
                                            <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-400 block mt-0.5">
                                                     RFQ {bid.rfqNo} | {bid.sku} ({bid.quotedQty} {bid.uom})
                                            </span>
                                        </td>
                                        <td className="py-3.5 px-3">
                                            <p className="font-semibold text-blue-700">{bid.supplierName}</p>
                                            <p className="text-[11px] text-blue-600/80">
                                                ({bid.supplierCode})
                                            </p>
                                        </td>
                                        <td className="py-3.5 px-3 max-w-[180px] text-[11px] text-slate-600 leading-snug">
                                            {bid.address}
                                        </td>
                                        <td className="py-3.5 px-3 whitespace-nowrap">
                        <span
                            className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] font-bold ${
                                bid.status === 'Awarded'
                                    ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border border-emerald-300'
                                    : bid.status === 'Completed'
                                        ? 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-500'
                                        : 'bg-blue-50 text-slate-800'
                            }`}
                        >
                          {bid.status}
                        </span>
                                        </td>
                                        <td className="py-3.5 px-3 whitespace-nowrap">
                        <span
                            className={`text-xs font-extrabold ${
                                isLowestNet ? 'text-emerald-700' : 'text-slate-800'
                            }`}
                        >
                          {(bid.totalNetValueRM * exchangeRate).toFixed(2)} {currency}
                        </span>
                                            <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">
                                                {sym} {(bid.unitPriceRM * exchangeRate).toFixed(2)} / {bid.uom}
                                            </p>
                                        </td>
                                        <td className="py-3.5 px-3 whitespace-nowrap font-mono text-[11px] text-slate-600">
                                            {bid.quotationDate}
                                        </td>
                                        <td className="py-3.5 px-3 text-center font-semibold text-slate-700">
                                            {bid.fullyQuotedItems}
                                        </td>
                                        <td className="py-3.5 px-3 text-center font-semibold text-slate-700">
                                            {isLowestNet ? '1/1' : '0/1'}
                                        </td>
                                        <td className="py-3.5 px-3 max-w-[240px]">
                                            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                          <span
                              className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-extrabold inline-flex items-center gap-1 ${
                                  bid.aiRecommendation === 'RECOMMENDED_BEST_VALUE'
                                      ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border border-emerald-300'
                                      : bid.aiRecommendation ===
                                      'LOWEST_PRICE_HIGH_RISK' ||
                                      bid.aiRecommendation === 'AVOID_DEFECT_RISK'
                                          ? 'bg-rose-100 text-rose-800 border border-rose-300'
                                          : 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800'
                              }`}
                          >
                            <Sparkles className="w-2.5 h-2.5" />
                            AI Score: {bid.aiQualityScore}/100
                          </span>
                                                <span
                                                    className={`text-[10px] font-bold ${
                                                        bid.pastDefectRatePct >= 5
                                                            ? 'text-rose-600'
                                                            : 'text-emerald-700'
                                                    }`}
                                                >
                            ({bid.pastDefectRatePct}% Defects)
                          </span>
                                            </div>
                                            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-600 mt-1 leading-snug">
                                                {bid.pastExperienceNote}
                                            </p>
                                            <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-800 mt-0.5">
                                                {bid.aiAnalysisNote}
                                            </p>
                                        </td>
                                        <td className="py-3.5 px-3 max-w-[220px]">
                                            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-1">
                          <span
                              className={`px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-bold ${
                                  bid.sampleTestStatus === 'PASSED'
                                      ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800'
                                      : bid.sampleTestStatus === 'FAILED'
                                          ? 'bg-rose-100 text-rose-800'
                                          : 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800'
                              }`}
                          >
                            Sample: {bid.sampleTestStatus}
                          </span>
                                                <button
                                                    type="button"
                                                    onClick={() => {
                                                        setReviewModalBid(bid);
                                                        setSampleStatusInput(bid.sampleTestStatus);
                                                        setDefectRateInput(String(bid.pastDefectRatePct));
                                                        setStaffCommentInput(bid.staffSampleComment);
                                                        setPastExpInput(bid.pastExperienceNote);
                                                        setStaffNameInput(bid.testedByStaff);
                                                    }}
                                                    className="text-[10px] text-blue-600 hover:underline font-bold inline-flex items-center gap-0.5 cursor-pointer"
                                                >
                                                    <MessageSquarePlus className="w-3 h-3" /> Edit Sample Note
                                                </button>
                                            </div>
                                            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-700 italic mt-1 leading-snug">
                                                &ldquo;{bid.staffSampleComment}&rdquo;
                                            </p>
                                            <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
                                                By: {bid.testedByStaff}
                                            </p>
                                        </td>
                                        <td className="py-3.5 px-4 text-right whitespace-nowrap">
                                            {bid.status === 'Awarded' ? (
                                                <span className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 font-bold text-xs inline-flex items-center gap-1">
                            <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Awarded &amp; PO Sent
                          </span>
                                            ) : (
                                                <div className="flex flex-col items-end gap-1">
                                                    <button
                                                        type="button"
                                                        disabled={!canApprove}
                                                        onClick={() => void handleAwardQuotation(bid)}
                                                        className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold inline-flex items-center gap-1 transition cursor-pointer ${
                                                            bid.aiRecommendation === 'RECOMMENDED_BEST_VALUE'
                                                                ? 'bg-[#2BB656] hover:bg-emerald-600 text-white shadow-2xs'
                                                                : 'bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-700 text-white'
                                                        }`}
                                                    >
                                                        <Award className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                                                        {bid.aiRecommendation === 'RECOMMENDED_BEST_VALUE'
                                                            ? 'Award AI Best Pick'
                                                            : 'Award Quote'}
                                                    </button>
                                                    {bid.aiRecommendation === 'LOWEST_PRICE_HIGH_RISK' && (
                                                        <span className="text-[10px] text-rose-600 font-semibold flex items-center gap-0.5">
                                <ShieldAlert className="w-3 h-3" /> High Defect Risk
                              </span>
                                                    )}
                                                </div>
                                            )}
                                        </td>
                                    </tr>
                                );
                            })}
                            </tbody>
                        </table>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}

            {/* VIEW 2: PRODUCT CATALOG & RESTOCK TABLE */}
            {viewMode === 'catalog' && (
                <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs overflow-hidden">
                    {showArchivedOnly && (
                        <div className="px-5 py-3 bg-amber-50/80 border-b border-amber-200 flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-amber-900">
                            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                <Archive className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-700 shrink-0" />
                                <span>
                  <strong>Decommissioned History:</strong> Displaying retired products. Historical invoices and logs are preserved, while future sales and restocks are disabled.
                </span>
                            </div>
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => setShowArchivedOnly(false)}
                                className="text-amber-800 font-bold underline hover:text-amber-950 cursor-pointer"
                            >
                                Return to Active Catalog
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    )}

                    <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                        <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse">
                            <thead>
                            <tr className="border-b border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/80 text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-600 uppercase tracking-wider">
                                <th className="py-2.5 px-4">Product &amp; Spec</th>
                                <th className="py-2.5 px-3 whitespace-nowrap">SKU &amp; Profile</th>
                                <th className="py-2.5 px-3 whitespace-nowrap">Live Stock vs Min</th>
                                <th className="py-2.5 px-3 whitespace-nowrap">Record Sale / Usage</th>
                                <th className="py-2.5 px-3">Active Supplier &amp; Bids</th>
                                <th className="py-2.5 px-3 whitespace-nowrap">Selling Price ({currency})</th>
                                <th className="py-2.5 px-4 w-72">Restock Action</th>
                                <th className="py-2.5 px-3 text-center w-14">Remove</th>
                            </tr>
                            </thead>
                            <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100 text-xs">
                            {displayedProducts.length === 0 ? (
                                <tr>
                                    <td colSpan={8} className="py-8 text-center text-slate-400 italic">
                                        {showArchivedOnly
                                            ? 'No decommissioned products found.'
