                                            : 'No active products available.'}
                                    </td>
                                </tr>
                            ) : (
                                displayedProducts.map((item) => {
                                    const isArchived =
                                        item.isRemoved ||
                                        item.visibility === false ||
                                        (item as any).isArchived;
                                    const isLow = !isArchived && item.stock <= item.minThreshold;
                                    const isOutOfStock = !isArchived && item.stock <= 0;
                                    const currentUsageVal = usageInputs[item.id] ?? '3';
                                    const currentReorderVal =
                                        reorderInputs[item.id] ?? String(item.reorderQty);
                                    const isApproving = Boolean(approvingIds[item.id]);
                                    const isConfirming = Boolean(confirmingIds[item.id]);
                                    const itemBids = quotations.filter(
                                        (q) => q.productId === item.id
                                    );

                                    return (
                                        <tr
                                            key={item.id}
                                            className={`transition ${
                                                isArchived
                                                    ? 'bg-slate-50/60 opacity-80'
                                                    : 'hover:bg-slate-50/70'
                                            }`}
                                        >
                                            <td className="py-2.5 px-4 max-w-[210px]">
                                                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                                    {item.photoUrl && (
                                                        <img
                                                            src={item.photoUrl}
                                                            alt={item.name}
                                                            className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg object-cover border border-slate-200 shrink-0"
                                                        />
                                                    )}
                                                    <div className="min-w-0">
                                                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                                                            <p
                                                                className={`font-bold truncate ${
                                                                    isArchived
                                                                        ? 'text-slate-600 line-through'
                                                                        : 'text-slate-900'
                                                                }`}
                                                                title={item.name}
                                                            >
                                                                {item.name}
                                                            </p>
                                                            {isArchived && (
                                                                <span className="px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded text-[9px] font-bold bg-amber-100 text-amber-800">
                                    Retired
                                  </span>
                                                            )}
                                                        </div>
                                                        <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 truncate">
                                                            {item.batchNo
                                                                ? `Batch: ${item.batchNo} | Exp: ${item.expiryDate}`
                                                                : item.variantSpec || item.category}
                                                        </p>
                                                    </div>
                                                </div>
                                            </td>
                                            <td className="py-2.5 px-3 whitespace-nowrap">
                                                <p className="font-mono text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700">
                                                    {item.sku}
                                                </p>
                                                <span className="inline-block mt-0.5 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold bg-slate-100 text-slate-600">
                            {item.industry.replace(/_/g, ' ')}
                          </span>
                                            </td>
                                            <td className="py-2.5 px-3 whitespace-nowrap">
                                                {isArchived ? (
                                                    <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-400">
                              Decommissioned
                            </span>
                                                ) : (
                                                    <div>
                                                        <div>
                                <span
                                    className={`text-sm font-extrabold ${
                                        isLow ? 'text-rose-600' : 'text-slate-900'
                                    }`}
                                >
                                  {item.stock.toLocaleString()} {item.uom}
                                </span>
                                                            <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                                  {' '}
                                                                / Min {item.minThreshold}
                                </span>
                                                        </div>
                                                        {isOutOfStock ? (
                                                            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 mt-0.5 text-[10px] text-rose-700 bg-rose-100 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded font-bold">
                                  Out of Stock (0 Left)
                                </span>
                                                        ) : isLow ? (
                                                            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 mt-0.5 text-[10px] text-rose-600 font-bold">
                                  <AlertTriangle className="w-3 h-3" /> Low Stock Alert
                                </span>
                                                        ) : null}
                                                    </div>
                                                )}
                                            </td>
                                            <td className="py-2.5 px-3 whitespace-nowrap">
                                                {isArchived ? (
                                                    <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 italic">
                              Sales locked
                            </span>
                                                ) : isOutOfStock ? (
                                                    <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 italic">
                              Restock required
                            </span>
                                                ) : (
                                                    <div className="space-y-1">
                                                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                                                            <input
                                                                type="text"
                                                                inputMode="numeric"
                                                                value={currentUsageVal}
                                                                placeholder="Qty"
                                                                onChange={(e) => {
                                                                    setUsageInputs((p) => ({
                                                                        ...p,
                                                                        [item.id]: sanitizeInt(e.target.value, 4),
                                                                    }));
                                                                    setUsageErrors((p) => ({
                                                                        ...p,
                                                                        [item.id]: '',
                                                                    }));
                                                                }}
                                                                className="w-14 px-2 py-1 rounded-lg bg-white border border-slate-300 text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500"
                                                            />
                                                            <button
                                                                type="button"
                                                                onClick={() => handleRequestSaleConfirmation(item)}
                                                                className="px-2.5 py-1 bg-slate-900 hover:bg-emerald-600 text-white rounded-lg text-[11px] font-semibold inline-flex items-center gap-1 transition cursor-pointer"
                                                            >
                                                                <ShoppingBag className="w-3 h-3" /> Sell / Use
                                                            </button>
                                                        </div>
                                                        {usageErrors[item.id] && (
                                                            <p className="text-[10px] text-rose-600 font-semibold flex items-center gap-0.5">
                                                                <AlertCircle className="w-2.5 h-2.5" />{' '}
                                                                {usageErrors[item.id]}
                                                            </p>
                                                        )}
                                                    </div>
                                                )}
                                            </td>
                                            <td className="py-2.5 px-3 max-w-[200px]">
                                                <p className="font-semibold text-slate-800 truncate">
                                                    {item.supplierName}
                                                </p>
                                                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 mt-0.5">
                            <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 font-mono truncate">
                              {item.supplierContact}
                            </span>
                                                    {!isArchived && (
                                                        <button
                                                            type="button"
                                                            onClick={() => {
                                                                setSelectedRfqProductId(item.id);
                                                                setViewMode('quotations');
                                                            }}
                                                            className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-blue-50 hover:bg-blue-100 text-blue-700 border border-blue-200 text-[10px] font-bold shrink-0 cursor-pointer"
                                                        >
                                                            Quotes ({itemBids.length})
                                                        </button>
                                                    )}
                                                </div>
                                            </td>
                                            <td className="py-2.5 px-3 whitespace-nowrap font-bold text-slate-800">
                                                {sym}{' '}
                                                {(item.priceRM * exchangeRate).toLocaleString(
                                                    undefined,
                                                    {
                                                        minimumFractionDigits: 2,
                                                        maximumFractionDigits: 2,
                                                    }
                                                )}
                                                {item.unitCostRM && (
                                                    <span className="block text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-normal">
                              Cost: {sym}{' '}
                                                        {(item.unitCostRM * exchangeRate).toFixed(2)}
                            </span>
                                                )}
                                            </td>
                                            <td className="py-2.5 px-4">
                                                {isArchived ? (
                                                    <span className="px-2.5 py-1 bg-slate-100 text-slate-500 rounded-lg text-[11px] font-semibold inline-flex items-center gap-1">
                              <Lock className="w-3 h-3 text-slate-400" /> Restock Disabled
                            </span>
                                                ) : item.restockStatus ===
                                                'TRIGGERED_PENDING_APPROVAL' ? (
                                                    <div className="space-y-1.5">
                                                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                                <span className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-500">
                                  PO Qty:
                                </span>
                                                            <input
                                                                type="text"
                                                                inputMode="numeric"
                                                                value={currentReorderVal}
                                                                onChange={(e) => {
                                                                    setReorderInputs((p) => ({
                                                                        ...p,
                                                                        [item.id]: sanitizeInt(e.target.value, 5),
                                                                    }));
                                                                    setReorderErrors((p) => ({
                                                                        ...p,
                                                                        [item.id]: '',
                                                                    }));
                                                                }}
                                                                className="w-16 px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-white border border-amber-300 text-xs font-extrabold text-slate-900 focus:outline-none"
                                                            />
                                                            <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500">
                                  {item.uom}
                                </span>
                                                        </div>
                                                        {reorderErrors[item.id] && (
                                                            <p className="text-[10px] text-rose-600 font-semibold">
                                                                {reorderErrors[item.id]}
                                                            </p>
                                                        )}
                                                        <button
                                                            type="button"
                                                            disabled={isApproving}
                                                            onClick={() => void handleApprovePO(item)}
                                                            className={`w-full px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-[11px] font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 shadow-xs transition cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap disabled:opacity-60 ${
                                                                canApprove
                                                                    ? 'bg-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-600 text-white'
                                                                    : 'bg-slate-200 text-slate-600'
                                                            }`}
                                                        >
                                                            {canApprove ? (
                                                                <>
                                                                    {isApproving ? (
                                                                        <Loader2 className="w-3 h-3 animate-spin shrink-0" />
                                                                    ) : (
                                                                        <Send className="w-3 h-3 shrink-0" />
                                                                    )}
                                                                    {isApproving
                                                                        ? 'Sending PO...'
                                                                        : `Approve & Contact (+${
                                                                            currentReorderVal || 0
                                                                        } ${item.uom})`}
                                                                </>
                                                            ) : (
                                                                <>
                                                                    <Lock className="w-3 h-3 shrink-0" /> Locked: Owner Only
                                                                </>
                                                            )}
                                                        </button>
                                                    </div>
                                                ) : item.restockStatus === 'SUPPLIER_CONTACTED' ? (
                                                    <div className="space-y-1.5">
                                                        <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-bold bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border border-blue-200">
                                                            <Clock className="w-2.5 h-2.5" /> PO Sent
                                                        </div>
                                                        <button
                                                            type="button"
                                                            disabled={isConfirming}
                                                            onClick={() =>
                                                                void handleConfirmDeliveryClick(item)
                                                            }
                                                            className="w-full px-3 py-1.5 bg-[#2BB656] hover:bg-emerald-600 disabled:opacity-60 text-white rounded-lg text-[11px] font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 shadow-xs transition cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap"
                                                        >
                                                            {isConfirming ? (
                                                                <Loader2 className="w-3 h-3 animate-spin" />
                                                            ) : (
                                                                <CheckCircle2 className="w-3 h-3" />
                                                            )}
                                                            {isConfirming
                                                                ? 'Confirming...'
                                                                : `Confirm Delivery (+${item.reorderQty} ${item.uom})`}
                                                        </button>
                                                    </div>
                                                ) : item.restockStatus === 'CONFIRMED' ? (
                                                    <span className="px-2.5 py-1 bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 rounded-lg text-[11px] font-bold inline-flex items-center gap-1 whitespace-nowrap">
                              <CheckCircle2 className="w-3 h-3 text-emerald-600" />{' '}
                                                        Delivered &amp; Restocked
                            </span>
                                                ) : (
                                                    <span className="px-2.5 py-1 bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 rounded-lg text-[11px] font-semibold inline-flex items-center gap-1 whitespace-nowrap">
                              Healthy Stock
                            </span>
                                                )}
                                            </td>
                                            <td className="py-2.5 px-3 text-center whitespace-nowrap">
                                                {!isArchived && canApprove && (
                                                    <button
                                                        type="button"
                                                        title="Remove / Decommission Product"
                                                        onClick={() => setPendingDecommissionProduct(item)}
                                                        className="p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-50 rounded-lg transition cursor-pointer"
                                                    >
                                                        <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                                                    </button>
                                                )}
                                            </td>
                                        </tr>
                                    );
                                })
                            )}
                            </tbody>
                        </table>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}

            {/* Modal: Confirm Product Decommission / Removal */}
            {pendingDecommissionProduct && (
                <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4 z-50">
                    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 max-w-md w-full p-6 shadow-2xl space-y-4">
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-100 pb-3">
                            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-rose-50 text-rose-600 flex items-center justify-center">
                                    <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4" />
                                </div>
                                <div>
                                    <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">
                                        Remove Product from Active Catalog
                                    </h3>
                                    <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">
                                        Historical records are preserved
                                    </p>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => setPendingDecommissionProduct(null)}
                                className="p-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 cursor-pointer"
                            >
                                <X className="w-4 h-4" />
                            </button>
                        </div>

                        <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 space-y-2.5 text-xs">
                            <div>
                <span className="text-slate-500 block text-[11px]">
                  Product to be retired:
                </span>
                                <strong className="text-slate-900 text-sm">
                                    {pendingDecommissionProduct.name} (
                                    {pendingDecommissionProduct.sku})
                                </strong>
                            </div>
                            <div className="p-3 bg-amber-50 rounded-lg border border-amber-200 text-amber-900 text-[11px] space-y-1">
                                <p className="font-bold">What happens next?</p>
                                <ul className="list-disc list-inside space-y-0.5 text-slate-700">
                                    <li>
                                        <strong>Past Invoices &amp; Logs:</strong> Kept intact in history.
                                    </li>
                                    <li>
                                        <strong>Future Revenue &amp; Sales:</strong> Excluded from new orders and future sales projections.
                                    </li>
                                    <li>
                                        <strong>Stock Actions:</strong> Restocking and manual sales deductions are disabled.
                                    </li>
                                </ul>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <div className="flex justify-end gap-2.5 pt-2 border-t border-slate-100">
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => setPendingDecommissionProduct(null)}
                                className="px-4 py-2 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold cursor-pointer"
                            >
                                Cancel
                            </button>
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => {
                                    onRemoveProduct?.(pendingDecommissionProduct.id);
                                    setPendingDecommissionProduct(null);
                                }}
                                className="px-4 py-2 bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-700 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-bold shadow-xs cursor-pointer"
                            >
                                Confirm Removal
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}

            {/* Modal: Log Warehouse Sample Test */}
            {reviewModalBid && (
                <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4 z-50">
                    <form
                        onSubmit={handleSaveSampleReview}
                        className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 max-w-lg w-full p-6 shadow-2xl space-y-4 text-xs"
                    >
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-100 pb-3">
                            <div>
                <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-blue-100 text-blue-800 font-mono text-[10px] font-bold">
                  Quotation {reviewModalBid.quotationNo}
                </span>
                                <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 mt-1">
                                    Warehouse Sample Test &amp; Past Defect Review (
                                    {reviewModalBid.supplierName})
                                </h3>
                            </div>
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => setReviewModalBid(null)}
                                className="p-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 cursor-pointer"
                            >
                                <X className="w-4 h-4" />
                            </button>
                        </div>

                        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                            <div>
                                <label className="font-bold text-slate-700 block mb-1">
                                    Sample Test Outcome
                                </label>
                                <select
                                    value={sampleStatusInput}
                                    onChange={(e) =>
                                        setSampleStatusInput(
                                            e.target.value as SupplierQuotationBid['sampleTestStatus']
                                        )
                                    }
                                    className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-white border border-slate-300 font-bold text-slate-900"
                                >
                                    <option value="PASSED">PASSED (Meets Quality Spec)</option>
                                    <option value="WARNING">WARNING (Minor Issue)</option>
                                    <option value="FAILED">FAILED (Defective Sample)</option>
                                </select>
                            </div>
                            <div>
                                <label className="font-bold text-slate-700 block mb-1">
                                    Past Defect Rate (%)
                                </label>
                                <input
                                    type="number"
                                    step="0.1"
                                    min="0"
                                    max="100"
                                    value={defectRateInput}
                                    onChange={(e) => setDefectRateInput(e.target.value)}
                                    className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-white border border-slate-300 font-bold text-slate-900"
                                />
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <div>
                            <label className="font-bold text-slate-700 block mb-1">
                                Past Purchase / Batch Defect Memory
                            </label>
                            <input
                                type="text"
                                value={pastExpInput}
                                onChange={(e) => setPastExpInput(e.target.value)}
                                placeholder="e.g., Previous batch had 8% broken seals or all 12 deliveries arrived intact"
                                className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-white border border-slate-300 text-slate-800"
                            />
                        </div>

                        <div>
                            <label className="font-bold text-slate-700 block mb-1">
                                Warehouse Staff Sample Testing Comment
                            </label>
                            <textarea
                                rows={2}
                                value={staffCommentInput}
                                onChange={(e) => setStaffCommentInput(e.target.value)}
                                placeholder="Enter staff observation after inspecting the supplier sample..."
                                className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-white border border-slate-300 text-slate-800"
                            />
                        </div>

                        <div>
                            <label className="font-bold text-slate-700 block mb-1">
                                Inspected By (Warehouse Staff)
                            </label>
                            <input
                                type="text"
                                value={staffNameInput}
                                onChange={(e) => setStaffNameInput(e.target.value)}
                                className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-white border border-slate-300 text-slate-800"
                            />
                        </div>

                        <div className="flex justify-end gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-100">
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => setReviewModalBid(null)}
                                className="px-4 py-2 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 rounded-xl font-semibold cursor-pointer"
                            >
                                Cancel
                            </button>
                            <button
                                type="submit"
                                className="px-4 py-2 bg-[#2BB656] hover:bg-emerald-600 text-white rounded-xl font-bold cursor-pointer"
                            >
                                Save &amp; Recalculate AI Score
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    </form>
                </div>
            )}

            {/* Modal: Add Supplier Quotation */}
            {showNewBidModal && (
                <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4 z-50">
                    <form
                        onSubmit={handleAddSupplierBid}
                        className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 max-w-lg w-full p-6 shadow-2xl space-y-4 text-xs"
                    >
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-100 pb-3">
                            <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">
                                Add Supplier Quotation Bid
                            </h3>
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => setShowNewBidModal(false)}
                                className="p-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 cursor-pointer"
                            >
                                <X className="w-4 h-4" />
                            </button>
                        </div>

                        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                            <div className="col-span-2">
                                <label className="font-bold text-slate-700 block mb-1">
                                    Target Product SKU *
                                </label>
                                <select
                                    value={newBidProductId}
                                    onChange={(e) => setNewBidProductId(e.target.value)}
                                    className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-white border border-slate-300 font-semibold text-slate-900"
                                >
                                    {activeProducts.map((p) => (
                                        <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>
                                            {p.name} ({p.sku}) â€” Reorder Qty: {p.reorderQty} {p.uom}
                                        </option>
                                    ))}
                                </select>
                            </div>

                            <div>
                                <label className="font-bold text-slate-700 block mb-1">
                                    Supplier Name *
                                </label>
                                <input
                                    type="text"
                                    required
                                    placeholder="e.g., Selangor Packaging Sdn Bhd"
                                    value={newBidSupplierName}
                                    onChange={(e) => setNewBidSupplierName(e.target.value)}
                                    className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-white border border-slate-300 text-slate-900"
                                />
                            </div>

                            <div>
                                <label className="font-bold text-slate-700 block mb-1">
                                    Supplier Code
                                </label>
                                <input
                                    type="text"
                                    value={newBidSupplierCode}
                                    onChange={(e) => setNewBidSupplierCode(e.target.value)}
                                    className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-white border border-slate-300 font-mono text-slate-900"
                                />
                            </div>

                            <div>
                                <label className="font-bold text-slate-700 block mb-1">
                                    Quoted Unit Price ({currency}) *
                                </label>
                                <input
                                    type="number"
                                    step="0.01"
                                    required
                                    placeholder="e.g., 38.00"
                                    value={newBidUnitPrice}
                                    onChange={(e) => setNewBidUnitPrice(e.target.value)}
                                    className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-white border border-slate-300 font-bold text-slate-900"
                                />
                            </div>

                            <div>
                                <label className="font-bold text-slate-700 block mb-1">
                                    Past Defect Rate (%)
                                </label>
                                <input
                                    type="number"
                                    step="0.1"
                                    value={newBidDefectPct}
                                    onChange={(e) => setNewBidDefectPct(e.target.value)}
                                    className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-white border border-slate-300 text-slate-900"
                                />
                            </div>

                            <div>
                                <label className="font-bold text-slate-700 block mb-1">
                                    Sample Test Status
                                </label>
                                <select
                                    value={newBidSampleStatus}
                                    onChange={(e) =>
                                        setNewBidSampleStatus(
                                            e.target.value as SupplierQuotationBid['sampleTestStatus']
                                        )
                                    }
                                    className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-white border border-slate-300 font-semibold text-slate-900"
                                >
                                    <option value="PASSED">PASSED</option>
                                    <option value="WARNING">WARNING</option>
                                    <option value="FAILED">FAILED</option>
                                </select>
                            </div>

                            <div>
                                <label className="font-bold text-slate-700 block mb-1">
                                    Supplier WhatsApp
                                </label>
                                <input
                                    type="text"
                                    value={newBidPhone}
                                    onChange={(e) => setNewBidPhone(e.target.value)}
                                    className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-white border border-slate-300 text-slate-900"
                                />
                            </div>

                            <div className="col-span-2">
                                <label className="font-bold text-slate-700 block mb-1">
                                    Supplier Address
                                </label>
                                <input
                                    type="text"
                                    placeholder="e.g., 25 Jalan Perusahaan, Shah Alam"
                                    value={newBidAddress}
                                    onChange={(e) => setNewBidAddress(e.target.value)}
                                    className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-white border border-slate-300 text-slate-900"
                                />
                            </div>

                            <div className="col-span-2">
                                <label className="font-bold text-slate-700 block mb-1">
                                    Staff Sample Comment
                                </label>
                                <input
                                    type="text"
                                    placeholder="e.g., Sample inspected by warehouse team; passed quality check."
                                    value={newBidComment}
                                    onChange={(e) => setNewBidComment(e.target.value)}
                                    className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-white border border-slate-300 text-slate-900"
                                />
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <div className="flex justify-end gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-100">
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => setShowNewBidModal(false)}
                                className="px-4 py-2 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 rounded-xl font-semibold cursor-pointer"
                            >
                                Cancel
                            </button>
                            <button
                                type="submit"
                                className="px-4 py-2 bg-[#2BB656] hover:bg-emerald-600 text-white rounded-xl font-bold cursor-pointer"
                            >
                                Save Quotation Bid
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    </form>
                </div>
            )}

            {/* Modal: Confirm Stock Deduction */}
            {pendingSaleConfirm && (
                <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4 z-50">
                    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 max-w-md w-full p-6 shadow-2xl space-y-4">
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-100 pb-3">
                            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600 flex items-center justify-center">
                                    <ShoppingBag className="w-4 h-4" />
                                </div>
                                <div>
                                    <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">
                                        Confirm Stock Deduction &amp; Sale
                                    </h3>
                                    <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">
                                        Review stock balance before updating inventory
                                    </p>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => setPendingSaleConfirm(null)}
                                className="p-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 cursor-pointer"
                            >
                                <X className="w-4 h-4" />
                            </button>
                        </div>

                        <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 space-y-2.5 text-xs">
                            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                                <span className="text-slate-500">Product SKU:</span>
                                <strong className="text-slate-900">
                                    {pendingSaleConfirm.item.name} ({pendingSaleConfirm.item.sku})
                                </strong>
                            </div>
                            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                                <span className="text-slate-500">Quantity to Deduct:</span>
                                <strong className="text-rose-600 text-sm">
                                    -{pendingSaleConfirm.qty} {pendingSaleConfirm.item.uom}
                                </strong>
                            </div>
                            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                                <span className="text-slate-500">Warehouse Stock Balance:</span>
                                <span className="font-bold text-slate-900">
                  {pendingSaleConfirm.item.stock} {pendingSaleConfirm.item.uom}{' '}
                                    &rarr;{' '}
                                    <strong
                                        className={
                                            pendingSaleConfirm.item.stock - pendingSaleConfirm.qty <=
                                            pendingSaleConfirm.item.minThreshold
                                                ? 'text-rose-600'
                                                : 'text-emerald-700'
                                        }
                                    >
                    {Math.max(
                        0,
                        pendingSaleConfirm.item.stock - pendingSaleConfirm.qty
                    )}{' '}
                                        {pendingSaleConfirm.item.uom}
                  </strong>
                </span>
                            </div>
                            <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-t border-slate-200 pt-2">
                                <span className="text-slate-500">Sales Invoice Amount:</span>
                                <strong className="text-emerald-700 text-sm">
                                    {sym}{' '}
                                    {(
                                        pendingSaleConfirm.qty *
                                        pendingSaleConfirm.item.priceRM *
                                        exchangeRate
                                    ).toLocaleString(undefined, {
                                        minimumFractionDigits: 2,
                                        maximumFractionDigits: 2,
                                    })}
                                </strong>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <div className="flex justify-end gap-2.5 pt-2 border-t border-slate-100">
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => setPendingSaleConfirm(null)}
                                className="px-4 py-2 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold cursor-pointer"
                            >
                                Cancel
                            </button>
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => {
                                    onRecordStockUsage(
                                        pendingSaleConfirm.item.id,
                                        pendingSaleConfirm.qty
                                    );
                                    setPendingSaleConfirm(null);
                                }}
                                className="px-4 py-2 bg-[#2BB656] hover:bg-emerald-600 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-bold shadow-xs cursor-pointer"
                            >
                                Confirm &amp; Deduct Stock
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}
        </div>
    );
};

import React, { useState, useEffect, useMemo, useRef } from 'react';

import { CURRENCY_SYMBOLS } from '../../types/vyte.ts';
import {getDebugMockMode, orbyteApi} from '../../services/api';
import { VyteDropdown } from '../../components/common/VyteDropdown';

interface OrderStatusModuleProps {
    products: Product[];
    role: UserRole;
    userID: string;
    currency?: CurrencyCode;
    exchangeRate?: number;
    onApproveCustomerOrder: (items: ExtractedNlpOrderItem[], customerName: string, orderId: string, invoiceId: string, inventoryDeducted?: boolean) => void;
    onTriggerShortageRestock?: (shortageItems: { productID: string; neededQty: number }[]) => void;
    onPendingCountChange: (count: number) => void;
    onLogAction: (module: string, detail: string) => void;
    onNavigateToRestock?: () => void;
    onNotify?: (type: 'success' | 'error' | 'warning' | 'info', message: string) => void;
    onTriggerLoadingScreen?: (message: string, durationMs?: number) => void;
}

type OrderFulfillmentStatus = 'PENDING_HUMAN_APPROVAL' | 'MODIFIED' | 'APPROVED_AWAITING_DELIVERY' | 'DELIVERED_COMPLETED' | 'REJECTED';

interface CustomerOrderRecord {
    orderID: string; invoiceID: string; invoicePdfUrl: string; customerName: string;
    requestedDate: string; rawMessage: string; internalNotes?: string; approvalNotes?: string;
    inventoryDeducted?: boolean; createdAt?: string; updatedAt?: string;
    items: ExtractedNlpOrderItem[]; subtotal: number; status: OrderFulfillmentStatus;
}

interface ParsedOrderDraft {
    customerName: string; requestedDate: string; rawMessage: string;
    internalNotes: string; items: ExtractedNlpOrderItem[]; currency: string;
}

const ORDER_DRAFT_KEY = 'vyte_order_parsed_step2_draft_v2';
const ORDER_NOTES_KEY = 'vyte_order_notes_map_v2';

            const currentAvail = liveProd ? liveProd.stock : item.availableStock;
