                            type="text"
                            inputMode="numeric"
                            placeholder="e.g., 3"
                            value={daysLeftInput}
                            onChange={(e) => {
                                setDaysLeftInput(sanitizePositiveInt(e.target.value));
                                if (errors.daysLeft) setErrors({ ...errors, daysLeft: '' });
                            }}
                            className={`w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-white border ${
                                errors.daysLeft ? 'border-rose-500' : 'border-slate-300'
                            }`}
                        />
                        {errors.daysLeft && (
                            <p className="text-[10px] text-rose-600 font-semibold mt-1 flex items-center gap-1">
                                <AlertCircle className="w-3 h-3" /> {errors.daysLeft}
                            </p>
                        )}
                    </div>

                    <div className="md:col-span-3">
                        <label className="font-bold text-slate-700 block mb-1">
                            Quality Defect / Expiry Trigger Reason *
                        </label>
                        <input
                            type="text"
                            placeholder="e.g., Seal moisture detected or expires in 72 hours"
                            value={triggerReason}
                            onChange={(e) => {
                                setTriggerReason(e.target.value);
                                if (errors.triggerReason)
                                    setErrors({ ...errors, triggerReason: '' });
                            }}
                            className={`w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-white border ${
                                errors.triggerReason ? 'border-rose-500' : 'border-slate-300'
                            }`}
                        />
                        {errors.triggerReason && (
                            <p className="text-[10px] text-rose-600 font-semibold mt-1 flex items-center gap-1">
                                <AlertCircle className="w-3 h-3" /> {errors.triggerReason}
                            </p>
                        )}
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex items-end">
                        <button
                            type="submit"
                            className="w-full py-2 px-4 bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-700 text-white font-bold rounded-lg transition cursor-pointer"
                        >
                            Add to QC Table
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </form>
            )}

            <div className="px-5 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center bg-slate-100 p-1 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 text-xs font-semibold">
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setStatusFilter('ALL')}
                        className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition cursor-pointer ${
                            statusFilter === 'ALL'
                                ? 'bg-white text-slate-900 shadow-2xs font-bold'
                                : 'text-slate-600'
                        }`}
                    >
                        All ({qcAlerts.length})
                    </button>
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setStatusFilter('PENDING_APPROVAL')}
                        className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition cursor-pointer ${
                            statusFilter === 'PENDING_APPROVAL'
                                ? 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-900 font-bold'
                                : 'text-slate-600'
                        }`}
                    >
                        Needs Staff Assignment (
                        {qcAlerts.filter((q) => q.status === 'PENDING_APPROVAL').length})
                    </button>
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setStatusFilter('ASSIGNED_TO_STAFF')}
                        className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition cursor-pointer ${
                            statusFilter === 'ASSIGNED_TO_STAFF'
                                ? 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-900 font-bold'
                                : 'text-slate-600'
                        }`}
                    >
                        Assigned to Warehouse (
                        {qcAlerts.filter((q) => q.status === 'ASSIGNED_TO_STAFF').length})
                    </button>
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setStatusFilter('OVERDUE_HR')}
                        className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition cursor-pointer ${
                            statusFilter === 'OVERDUE_HR'
                                ? 'bg-rose-600 text-white font-bold'
                                : overdueCount > 0
                                    ? 'text-rose-600 font-bold'
                                    : 'text-slate-600'
                        }`}
                    >
                        Overdue Escalated ({overdueCount})
                    </button>
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setStatusFilter('APPROVED_RESOLVED')}
                        className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition cursor-pointer ${
                            statusFilter === 'APPROVED_RESOLVED'
                                ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-900 font-bold'
                                : 'text-slate-600'
                        }`}
                    >
                        Completed (
                        {qcAlerts.filter((q) => q.status === 'APPROVED_RESOLVED').length})
                    </button>
                </div>

                <div className="relative w-64">
                    <Search className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400 absolute left-3 top-2.5" />
                    <input
                        type="text"
                        placeholder="Filter batch, SKU or staff..."
                        value={searchQuery}
                        onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full pl-8 pr-3 py-1.5 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-xs focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500"
                    />
                </div>
            </div>

            <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse">
                    <thead>
                    <tr className="border-b border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/70 text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-600 uppercase">
                        <th className="py-3 px-4">Batch &amp; SKU</th>
                        <th className="py-3 px-2.5">Risk Level</th>
                        <th className="py-3 px-2.5">Product &amp; Trigger Reason</th>
                        <th className="py-3 px-2.5">AI Recommendation (Click-In)</th>
                        <th className="py-3 px-2.5">Loss Preventable ({currency})</th>
                        <th className="py-3 px-2.5">Warehouse Assignee</th>
                        <th className="py-3 px-2.5">Task Due Date &amp; SLA</th>
                        <th className="py-3 px-4">Warehouse Task Action</th>
                    </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100 text-xs">
                    {filteredAlerts.map((qc) => {
                        const isUrgent = qc.daysLeft <= 5;
                        const convertedSaved = (qc.savedRM * exchangeRate).toFixed(2);
                        const isOverdueActive =
                            qc.status === 'ASSIGNED_TO_STAFF' &&
                            Boolean(qc.assignedDueDate && qc.assignedDueDate < todayIso);
                        const wasCompletedLate = isCompletedPastDue(qc);

                        return (
                            <tr
                                key={qc.id}
                                className={`transition ${
                                    isOverdueActive
                                        ? 'bg-rose-50/35 hover:bg-rose-50/60'
                                        : 'hover:bg-slate-50/80'
                                }`}
                            >
                                <td className="py-3.5 px-4 whitespace-nowrap">
                                    <p className="font-mono font-bold text-slate-900">
                                        {qc.batchNo}
                                    </p>
                                    <span className="inline-block mt-0.5 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-bold bg-slate-100 text-slate-700">
                      {qc.sku} â€¢ {qc.qtyAffected}
                    </span>
                                </td>

                                <td className="py-3.5 px-2.5 whitespace-nowrap">
                    <span
                        className={`px-2 py-1 rounded-md text-[11px] font-bold inline-flex items-center gap-1 ${
                            isUrgent
                                ? 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 border border-rose-200'
                                : 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-800 border border-amber-200'
                        }`}
                    >
                      <AlertTriangle className="w-3 h-3" />
                        {isUrgent ? `${qc.daysLeft}d Left (Urgent)` : 'Batch Defect'}
                    </span>
                                </td>

                                <td className="py-3.5 px-2.5 max-w-[190px]">
                                    <p
                                        className="font-bold text-slate-900 truncate"
                                        title={qc.productName}
                                    >
                                        {qc.productName}
                                    </p>
                                    <p
                                        className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 truncate"
                                        title={qc.triggerReason}
                                    >
                                        {qc.triggerReason}
                                    </p>
                                </td>

                                <td className="py-3.5 px-2.5">
                                    <button
                                        type="button"
                                        onClick={() => openAiRecommendationModal(qc)}
                                        className="px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-purple-50 hover:bg-purple-100 border border-purple-200 text-purple-800 font-bold text-xs inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 transition cursor-pointer max-w-[215px]"
                                    >
                                        <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-purple-600 shrink-0" />
                                        <span className="truncate">{qc.actionLabel}</span>
                                        <span className="bg-purple-200/80 text-purple-900 text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded font-extrabold shrink-0">
                        Click-In
                      </span>
                                    </button>
                                </td>

                                <td className="py-3.5 px-2.5 font-extrabold text-emerald-700 whitespace-nowrap">
                                    {currencySymbol}{' '}
                                    {Number(convertedSaved).toLocaleString(undefined, {
                                        minimumFractionDigits: 2,
                                    })}
                                </td>

                                    {qc.assignedStaffName ? (

            {inspectingPayload && (
                <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/65 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4 z-50">

                        <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3 border-b border-slate-800 pb-3">
                            <div>
                <span className="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-300 border border-rose-500/40 uppercase">

                </span>

                                    Overdue Warehouse Task Escalation Payload
                                </h3>
                                <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">
                                    Dispatched because task{' '}
                                    <strong>{inspectingPayload.hrTaskId}</strong> remained undone
                                    past its due date (
                                    <strong>
                                        {inspectingPayload.taskDetails.assignedDueDate}
                                    </strong>
                                    ).
                                </p>
                            </div>
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => setInspectingPayload(null)}
                                className="p-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-white cursor-pointer"
                            >
                                <X className="w-4 h-4" />
                            </button>
                        </div>

                        <div className="relative">
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => {
                                    void navigator.clipboard.writeText(
                                        JSON.stringify(inspectingPayload, null, 2)
                                    );
                                    setCopiedPayload(true);
                                    setTimeout(() => setCopiedPayload(false), 2000);
                                }}
                                className="absolute right-3 top-3 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-xs font-semibold text-emerald-400 flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer border border-slate-700"

                                        <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Copied
                                    </>
                                ) : (
                                    <>
                                        <Copy className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Copy JSON
                                    </>
                                )}
                            </button>
                            <pre className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-[#0B0C0E] border border-slate-800 text-[11px] font-mono text-emerald-300 overflow-x-auto max-h-80 leading-relaxed">
                {JSON.stringify(inspectingPayload, null, 2)}
              </pre>
                        </div>

                        <div className="flex justify-end pt-2 border-t border-slate-800">
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => setInspectingPayload(null)}
                                className="px-4 py-2 bg-[#2BB656] hover:bg-emerald-500 text-slate-950 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl cursor-pointer"
                            >
                                Close Payload Inspector
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}
        </div>
    );
};

import React, { useState, useRef, useMemo } from 'react';
import {
    Plus, CheckCircle2, FileText, AlertCircle, Sparkles,
    ArrowUpRight, ArrowDownLeft, X, Camera, FileSpreadsheet, Upload,
    Wallet, ShieldAlert, TrendingUp, Download,
} from 'lucide-react';
import type { CurrencyCode, InvoiceItem, Product, SstTaxCode, UserRole } from '../../types/vyte.ts';
import { CURRENCY_SYMBOLS } from '../../types/vyte.ts';
import { getDebugMockMode, orbyteApi } from '../../services/api.ts';

interface InvoiceModuleProps {
    invoices: InvoiceItem[];
    products?: Product[];
    role: UserRole;
    currency?: CurrencyCode;
    exchangeRate?: number;
    onVerifyInvoice: (invId: string, updatedCategory?: string) => void;
    onToggleInvoicePaid: (invId: string) => void;
    onAddValidatedInvoice: (createdInv: InvoiceItem) => void;
    onBatchImportInvoices?: (importedInvoices: InvoiceItem[]) => void;
}

const ACCOUNTING_CATEGORIES = [
    'Inventory Procurement (Raw Materials)',
    'Packaging & Sustainable Supplies',
    'Cold-Chain Logistics & Freight',
    'Wholesale B2B Product Sales',
    'Direct Product Sales Revenue',
    'Store Utilities & Equipment Maintenance',
];

const SST_TAX_OPTIONS: { code: SstTaxCode; label: string; ratePct: number; badgeCls: string }[] = [
    { code: 'EXEMPT_0', label: '0% SST Exempt (Basic Food / Export)', ratePct: 0, badgeCls: 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-700' },
    { code: 'FNB_LOGISTICS_6', label: '6% SST (F&B & Cold-Chain Logistics)', ratePct: 6, badgeCls: 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-800' },
    { code: 'SERVICE_8', label: '8% SST (RMCD Standard Service Tax)', ratePct: 8, badgeCls: 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-900' },
    { code: 'SALES_10', label: '10% SST (Manufactured Goods Sales Tax)', ratePct: 10, badgeCls: 'bg-purple-100 text-purple-800' },
];

const sanitizeDecimal = (raw: string): string => {
    const parts = raw.replace(/[^0-9.]/g, '').split('.');
    const intPart = parts[0].replace(/^0+/, '').slice(0, 7);
    return parts.length > 1 ? `${intPart || '0'}.${parts[1].slice(0, 2)}` : intPart;
};

export const InvoiceModule: React.FC<InvoiceModuleProps> = ({
                                                                invoices, products = [], role, currency = 'MYR', exchangeRate = 1.0,
                                                                onVerifyInvoice, onToggleInvoicePaid, onAddValidatedInvoice, onBatchImportInvoices,
                                                            }) => {
    const sym = CURRENCY_SYMBOLS[currency] || 'RM';
    const canApprove = role === 'SME_OWNER' || role === 'MANAGER';

    const [directionFilter, setDirectionFilter] = useState<'ALL' | 'SUPPLIER_PAYABLE' | 'CUSTOMER_RECEIVABLE'>('ALL');
    const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState<'ALL' | 'NEEDS_REVIEW' | 'VERIFIED'>('ALL');
    const [selectedCategories, setSelectedCategories] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
    const [selectedSstCodes, setSelectedSstCodes] = useState<Record<string, SstTaxCode>>({});
    const [showUploadForm, setShowUploadForm] = useState(false);

    // Cash Flow Liquidity Guard Interactive Inputs
    const [bankCashInputRM, setBankCashInputRM] = useState<string>('14500');
    const [monthlyFixedOpExRM, setMonthlyFixedOpExRM] = useState<string>('8500');

    const photoInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
    const excelInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
    const [photoPreviewUrl, setPhotoPreviewUrl] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const [uploadedFileName, setUploadedFileName] = useState<string | null>(null);

    const [invNo, setInvNo] = useState('');
    const [invType, setInvType] = useState<'SUPPLIER_PAYABLE' | 'CUSTOMER_RECEIVABLE'>('SUPPLIER_PAYABLE');
    const [partyName, setPartyName] = useState('');
    const [amountInput, setAmountInput] = useState('');
    const [newInvSstCode, setNewInvSstCode] = useState<SstTaxCode>('FNB_LOGISTICS_6');
    const [rawLineDescription, setRawLineDescription] = useState('');
    const [formErrors, setFormErrors] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});

    const inferSstForInvoice = (inv: InvoiceItem): { code: SstTaxCode; ratePct: number; sstRM: number } => {
        const overrideCode = selectedSstCodes[inv.id] ?? inv.sstCode;
        if (overrideCode) {
            const found = SST_TAX_OPTIONS.find((o) => o.code === overrideCode) || SST_TAX_OPTIONS[0];
            return { code: found.code, ratePct: found.ratePct, sstRM: Number(((inv.amountRM * found.ratePct) / 100).toFixed(2)) };
        }
        const cat = inv.aiCategory.toLowerCase();
        if (cat.includes('raw') || cat.includes('coffee') || cat.includes('milk')) {
            return { code: 'EXEMPT_0', ratePct: 0, sstRM: 0 };
        }
        if (cat.includes('utilities') || cat.includes('maintenance') || cat.includes('it')) {
            return { code: 'SERVICE_8', ratePct: 8, sstRM: Number((inv.amountRM * 0.08).toFixed(2)) };
        }
        return { code: 'FNB_LOGISTICS_6', ratePct: 6, sstRM: Number((inv.amountRM * 0.06).toFixed(2)) };
    };

    const filtered = invoices.filter((i) => (directionFilter === 'ALL' || i.type === directionFilter) && (statusFilter === 'ALL' || i.status === statusFilter));
    const totalPayablesRM = invoices.filter((i) => i.type === 'SUPPLIER_PAYABLE').reduce((s, i) => s + i.amountRM, 0);
    const totalReceivablesRM = invoices.filter((i) => i.type === 'CUSTOMER_RECEIVABLE').reduce((s, i) => s + i.amountRM, 0);

    const cashFlowAnalysis = useMemo(() => {
        const currentCashRM = Math.max(0, Number(bankCashInputRM) || 0);
        const fixedOverheadRM = Math.max(0, Number(monthlyFixedOpExRM) || 0);

        const unpaidSupplierBillsRM = invoices
            .filter((i) => i.type === 'SUPPLIER_PAYABLE' && !i.paid)
            .reduce((s, i) => s + i.amountRM + inferSstForInvoice(i).sstRM, 0);

        const pendingRestockPoRM = products
            .filter((p) => p.restockStatus === 'TRIGGERED_PENDING_APPROVAL')
            .reduce((s, p) => s + p.reorderQty * (p.unitCostRM ?? p.priceRM * 0.62), 0);

        const unpaidCustomerReceivablesRM = invoices
            .filter((i) => i.type === 'CUSTOMER_RECEIVABLE' && !i.paid)
            .reduce((s, i) => s + i.amountRM + inferSstForInvoice(i).sstRM, 0);

        const totalSstOutputTaxRM = invoices
            .filter((i) => i.type === 'CUSTOMER_RECEIVABLE')
            .reduce((s, i) => s + inferSstForInvoice(i).sstRM, 0);

        const total30DayOutflowRM = unpaidSupplierBillsRM + pendingRestockPoRM + fixedOverheadRM + totalSstOutputTaxRM;
        const minSafeCashNeededRM = Number((total30DayOutflowRM * 1.2).toFixed(2));
        const projectedLiquidCashRM = Number((currentCashRM + unpaidCustomerReceivablesRM * 0.8).toFixed(2));
        const cashGapOrSurplusRM = Number((projectedLiquidCashRM - minSafeCashNeededRM).toFixed(2));
        const dailyBurnRM = Math.max(100, total30DayOutflowRM / 30);
        const runwayDays = Math.round(projectedLiquidCashRM / dailyBurnRM);

        return {
            currentCashRM,
            fixedOverheadRM,
            unpaidSupplierBillsRM,
            pendingRestockPoRM,
            unpaidCustomerReceivablesRM,
            totalSstOutputTaxRM,
            total30DayOutflowRM,
            minSafeCashNeededRM,
            projectedLiquidCashRM,
            cashGapOrSurplusRM,
            runwayDays,
            isHealthy: cashGapOrSurplusRM >= 0,
        };
    }, [bankCashInputRM, monthlyFixedOpExRM, invoices, products, selectedSstCodes]);

    const handleExportSst02Csv = () => {
        const headers = ['Invoice No', 'Date', 'Direction', 'Counterparty', 'Category', 'SST Tax Code', 'SST Rate (%)', `Base Amount (${currency})`, `SST Tax (${currency})`, `Total Incl. SST (${currency})`];
        const rows = invoices.map((inv) => {
            const sst = inferSstForInvoice(inv);
            const base = inv.amountRM * exchangeRate;
            const tax = sst.sstRM * exchangeRate;
            return [inv.invNo, inv.date, inv.type, `"${inv.party}"`, `"${inv.aiCategory}"`, sst.code, `${sst.ratePct}%`, base.toFixed(2), tax.toFixed(2), (base + tax).toFixed(2)].join(',');
        });
        const blob = new Blob(['\uFEFF' + [headers.join(','), ...rows].join('\n')], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' });
        const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        const a = document.createElement('a');
        a.href = url; a.setAttribute('download', `Vyte_RMCD_SST02_Tax_Schedule_2026.csv`);
        document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); document.body.removeChild(a); URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    };

    const handleSendPaymentReminder = (inv: InvoiceItem, lang: 'EN' | 'BM' = 'EN') => {
        const sst = inferSstForInvoice(inv);
        const amountFormatted = `${sym} ${((inv.amountRM + sst.sstRM) * exchangeRate).toFixed(2)}`;
        const messages = {
            EN: `Hi ${inv.party}, friendly reminder from Vyte Billing: Invoice ${inv.invNo} (${amountFormatted} incl. SST) dated ${inv.date} is due. Pay securely via DuitNow/FPX: https://pay.vyte.my/${inv.invNo}`,
            BM: `Salam ${inv.party}, peringatan mesra: Invois ${inv.invNo} (${amountFormatted}) bertarikh ${inv.date} masih belum dijelaskan. Sila buat bayaran melalui DuitNow/FPX: https://pay.vyte.my/${inv.invNo}`,
        };
        window.open(`https://wa.me/60123456789?text=${encodeURIComponent(messages[lang])}`, '_blank', 'noopener,noreferrer');
    };

    const getSourceBadge = (inv: InvoiceItem) => {
        if (inv.invNo.startsWith('SUP-')) return { label: 'Auto-Restock PO', color: 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800' };
        if (inv.invNo.startsWith('SALE-')) return { label: 'Direct Counter Sale', color: 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800' };
        if (inv.invNo.startsWith('CUST-')) return { label: 'NLP Customer Order', color: 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-800' };
        if (inv.attachmentName?.endsWith('.csv') || inv.attachmentName?.endsWith('.xlsx')) return { label: `Excel: ${inv.attachmentName}`, color: 'bg-teal-100 text-teal-800' };
        if (inv.attachmentPreviewUrl) return { label: 'Photo OCR Extracted', color: 'bg-purple-100 text-purple-800' };
        return { label: 'Uploaded Invoice', color: 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-700' };
    };

    const handlePhotoUpload = async (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
        const file = e.target.files?.[0];
        if (!file) return;
        if (!getDebugMockMode()) {
            try {
                const created = await orbyteApi.uploadInvoiceFileOrText({ file, userID: 'M067' });
                if (created) { onAddValidatedInvoice(created); e.target.value = ''; return; }
            } catch (err) { console.error(err); }
        }
        const reader = new FileReader();
        reader.onload = () => {
            setPhotoPreviewUrl(String(reader.result));
            setUploadedFileName(file.name);
            setShowUploadForm(true);
            if (!invNo) setInvNo(`SUP-OCR-${Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000)}`);
            if (!partyName) setPartyName('Selangor Cold-Storage & Packaging Sdn Bhd');
            if (!amountInput) setAmountInput((780 * exchangeRate).toFixed(2));
            if (!rawLineDescription) setRawLineDescription(`AI OCR extracted from photo "${file.name}": 25 boxes insulated packaging & cold-chain delivery`);
            setFormErrors({});
        };
        reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    };

    const handleExcelSpreadsheetUpload = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
        const file = e.target.files?.[0];
        if (!file) return;
        const reader = new FileReader();
        reader.onload = () => {
            const lines = String(reader.result || '').split(/\r?\n/).map((l) => l.trim()).filter(Boolean);
            const imported: InvoiceItem[] = [];
            lines.forEach((line, idx) => {
                if (idx === 0 && line.toLowerCase().includes('invoice')) return;
                const cols = line.split(',').map((c) => c.trim().replace(/^"|"$/g, ''));
                if (cols.length >= 3) {
                    imported.push({
                        id: `INV-XLS-${Date.now()}-${idx}`, invNo: cols[0] || `XLS-2026-${100 + idx}`,
                        type: cols[1]?.toUpperCase() === 'CUSTOMER_RECEIVABLE' ? 'CUSTOMER_RECEIVABLE' : 'SUPPLIER_PAYABLE',
                        party: cols[2] || 'Spreadsheet Imported Partner', date: '22 Oct 2026', amountRM: Number(cols[3]) || 650.0,
                        sstCode: 'FNB_LOGISTICS_6', sstRatePct: 6, aiCategory: cols[4] || 'Inventory Procurement (Raw Materials)',
                        aiConfidence: 96, paid: false, status: 'NEEDS_REVIEW', attachmentName: file.name,
                    });
                }
            });
            if (!imported.length) {
                imported.push({
                    id: `INV-XLS-${Date.now()}-1`, invNo: `XLS-2026-${Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000)}`,
                    type: 'SUPPLIER_PAYABLE', party: 'Klang Valley Roasters Sdn Bhd', date: '22 Oct 2026', amountRM: 1480.0,
                    sstCode: 'EXEMPT_0', sstRatePct: 0, aiCategory: 'Inventory Procurement (Raw Materials)',
                    aiConfidence: 97, paid: false, status: 'NEEDS_REVIEW', attachmentName: file.name,
                });
            }
            if (onBatchImportInvoices) onBatchImportInvoices(imported);
            else imported.forEach(onAddValidatedInvoice);
            e.target.value = '';
        };
        reader.readAsText(file);
    };

    const handleUploadSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        const errs: Record<string, string> = {};
        if (!invNo.trim() || !/^[A-Za-z0-9-_]{4,20}$/.test(invNo.trim())) errs.invNo = 'Invoice No. must be 4â€“20 alphanumeric chars.';
        if (!partyName.trim() || partyName.trim().length < 3) errs.partyName = 'Counterparty business name is required.';
        const amt = Number(amountInput);
        if (!amountInput || isNaN(amt) || amt <= 0 || amt > 1000000) errs.amountInput = `Enter a valid amount.`;
        if (!rawLineDescription.trim() || rawLineDescription.trim().length < 6) errs.rawLineDescription = 'Describe invoice items for AI & SST classification.';
        if (Object.keys(errs).length > 0) return setFormErrors(errs);

        const lower = rawLineDescription.toLowerCase();
        const aiCategory = /box|pack|cup/.test(lower)
            ? 'Packaging & Sustainable Supplies'
            : /delivery|freight|cold/.test(lower)
                ? 'Cold-Chain Logistics & Freight'
                : /electric|chiller|repair/.test(lower)
                    ? 'Store Utilities & Equipment Maintenance'
                    : invType === 'SUPPLIER_PAYABLE'
                        ? 'Inventory Procurement (Raw Materials)'
                        : 'Wholesale B2B Product Sales';

        const sstObj = SST_TAX_OPTIONS.find((o) => o.code === newInvSstCode) || SST_TAX_OPTIONS[1];
        const baseRM = Number((amt / exchangeRate).toFixed(2));

        onAddValidatedInvoice({
            id: `INV-${Date.now()}`, invNo: invNo.trim().toUpperCase(), type: invType, party: partyName.trim(),
            date: '22 Oct 2026', amountRM: baseRM, sstCode: sstObj.code, sstRatePct: sstObj.ratePct,
            sstAmountRM: Number(((baseRM * sstObj.ratePct) / 100).toFixed(2)),
            aiCategory, aiConfidence: 96, paid: false, status: 'NEEDS_REVIEW',
            attachmentName: uploadedFileName || undefined, attachmentPreviewUrl: photoPreviewUrl || undefined,
        });
        setInvNo(''); setPartyName(''); setAmountInput(''); setRawLineDescription(''); setPhotoPreviewUrl(null); setUploadedFileName(null); setFormErrors({}); setShowUploadForm(false);
    };

    return (
        <div className="space-y-4">
            {/* CASH FLOW & WORKING CAPITAL LIQUIDITY GUARD */}
            <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-slate-900 via-[#10241B] to-slate-900 text-white rounded-2xl p-5 border border-emerald-500/30 shadow-md space-y-4">
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-white/10 pb-3">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                        <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-emerald-500/20 border border-emerald-400/40 text-emerald-300 flex items-center justify-center">
                            <Wallet className="w-5 h-5" />
                        </div>
                        <div>
                            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-white">
                                    Cash Flow Liquidity Guard: Minimum Safe Working Capital
                                </h3>
                                <span className={`px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-extrabold ${
                                    cashFlowAnalysis.isHealthy
                                        ? 'bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-400/40'
                                        : 'bg-rose-500/30 text-rose-200 border border-rose-400/50'
                                }`}>
                                    {cashFlowAnalysis.isHealthy
                                        ? `HEALTHY RUNWAY (${cashFlowAnalysis.runwayDays} Days Cover)`
                                        : `CASH SHORTFALL ALERT (${cashFlowAnalysis.runwayDays} Days Left)`}
                                </span>
                            </div>
                            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-300">
                                Calculates your exact <strong>Minimum Required Safe Cash</strong> for the next 30 days (Unpaid Bills + Pending POs + Fixed Overhead + SST).
                            </p>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* Interactive Inputs */}
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 text-xs">
                        <div className="bg-white/10 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl border border-white/15">
                            <label className="text-[10px] text-emerald-300 font-bold block">Current Bank Cash (RM)</label>
                            <input
                                type="text"
                                inputMode="numeric"
                                value={bankCashInputRM}
                                onChange={(e) => setBankCashInputRM(e.target.value.replace(/[^0-9]/g, ''))}
                                className="w-24 bg-transparent text-white font-extrabold text-xs focus:outline-none border-b border-emerald-400"
                            />
                        </div>
                        <div className="bg-white/10 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl border border-white/15">
                            <label className="text-[10px] text-amber-300 font-bold block">Monthly Rent &amp; Payroll (RM)</label>
                            <input
                                type="text"
                                inputMode="numeric"
                                value={monthlyFixedOpExRM}
                                onChange={(e) => setMonthlyFixedOpExRM(e.target.value.replace(/[^0-9]/g, ''))}
                                className="w-24 bg-transparent text-white font-extrabold text-xs focus:outline-none border-b border-amber-400"
                            />
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-4 gap-3 text-xs">
                    <div className="p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-white/5 border border-white/10 space-y-1">
                        <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase text-amber-300">1. Committed 30-Day Outflows</span>
                        <p className="text-base font-extrabold text-white">
                            {sym} {(cashFlowAnalysis.total30DayOutflowRM * exchangeRate).toLocaleString(undefined, { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 })}
                        </p>
                        <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-300">
                            Unpaid Bills ({sym} {(cashFlowAnalysis.unpaidSupplierBillsRM * exchangeRate).toFixed(0)}) + Pending POs ({sym} {(cashFlowAnalysis.pendingRestockPoRM * exchangeRate).toFixed(0)}) + OpEx
                        </p>
                    </div>

                    <div className="p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-emerald-500/15 border border-emerald-400/40 space-y-1">
                        <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase text-emerald-300">2. Minimum Safe Cash Needed</span>
                        <p className="text-base font-extrabold text-emerald-300">
                            {sym} {(cashFlowAnalysis.minSafeCashNeededRM * exchangeRate).toLocaleString(undefined, { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 })}
                        </p>
                        <p className="text-[11px] text-emerald-100">
                            Includes <strong>+20% Working Capital Safety Buffer</strong> to prevent stockout or payroll crunch.
                        </p>
                    </div>

                    <div className="p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-white/5 border border-white/10 space-y-1">
                        <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase text-blue-300">3. Cash + Collectable AR (80%)</span>
                        <p className="text-base font-extrabold text-white">
                            {sym} {(cashFlowAnalysis.projectedLiquidCashRM * exchangeRate).toLocaleString(undefined, { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 })}
                        </p>
                        <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-300">
                            Bank ({sym} {(cashFlowAnalysis.currentCashRM * exchangeRate).toFixed(0)}) + Unpaid AR ({sym} {(cashFlowAnalysis.unpaidCustomerReceivablesRM * exchangeRate).toFixed(0)})
                        </p>
                    </div>

                    <div className={`p-3.5 rounded-xl border space-y-1 ${
                        cashFlowAnalysis.isHealthy
                            ? 'bg-emerald-950/60 border-emerald-400/40'
                            : 'bg-rose-950/80 border-rose-400/60'
                    }`}>
                        <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase text-white flex items-center gap-1">
                            {cashFlowAnalysis.isHealthy ? <TrendingUp className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-400" /> : <ShieldAlert className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-rose-400" />}
                            4. Net Liquidity Position
                        </span>
                        <p className={`text-base font-extrabold ${cashFlowAnalysis.isHealthy ? 'text-emerald-300' : 'text-rose-300'}`}>
                            {cashFlowAnalysis.isHealthy ? '+' : ''}
                            {sym} {(cashFlowAnalysis.cashGapOrSurplusRM * exchangeRate).toLocaleString(undefined, { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 })}
                        </p>
                        <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-200">
                            {cashFlowAnalysis.isHealthy
                                ? 'Healthy runway. Available cash is sufficient to cover pending restock orders.'
                                : `Cash deficit of ${sym} ${(Math.abs(cashFlowAnalysis.cashGapOrSurplusRM) * exchangeRate).toFixed(0)}. Click "Remind Customer" below to expedite receivables.`}
                        </p>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* INVOICE & GOVERNMENT SST REPOSITORY */}
            <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs overflow-hidden space-y-4">
                <input ref={photoInputRef} type="file" accept="image/*" onChange={handlePhotoUpload} className="hidden" />
                <input ref={excelInputRef} type="file" accept=".csv,.xlsx,.xls" onChange={handleExcelSpreadsheetUpload} className="hidden" />

                <div className="px-5 py-4 border-b border-slate-100 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4">
                    <div>
                        <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-2">
                            <FileText className="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-600" />
                            Smart Invoices &amp; RMCD SST Compliance Repository (0% / 6% F&amp;B / 8% Service / 10% Sales Tax)
                        </h3>
                        <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
                            Automated RMCD SST computation, statutory invoice tracking, and customer AR reminders.
                        </p>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                        <div className="hidden xl:flex items-center gap-3 bg-slate-50 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 text-xs">
                            <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-amber-700 font-bold">
                                <ArrowUpRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Payables: {sym} {(totalPayablesRM * exchangeRate).toLocaleString(undefined, { minimumFractionDigits: 2 })}
                            </span>
                            <span className="text-slate-300">|</span>
                            <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-blue-700 font-bold">
                                <ArrowDownLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Receivables: {sym} {(totalReceivablesRM * exchangeRate).toLocaleString(undefined, { minimumFractionDigits: 2 })}
                            </span>
                        </div>
                        <button type="button" onClick={handleExportSst02Csv} className="px-3 py-2 bg-amber-50 hover:bg-amber-100 text-amber-900 border border-amber-200 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition cursor-pointer">
                            <Download className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-700" /> Export SST-02 Schedule
                        </button>
                        <button type="button" onClick={() => photoInputRef.current?.click()} className="px-3 py-2 bg-purple-50 hover:bg-purple-100 text-purple-800 border border-purple-200 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition cursor-pointer">
                            <Camera className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-purple-600" /> Upload Receipt Photo
                        </button>
                        <button type="button" onClick={() => excelInputRef.current?.click()} className="px-3 py-2 bg-teal-50 hover:bg-teal-100 text-teal-800 border border-teal-200 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition cursor-pointer">
                            <FileSpreadsheet className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-teal-600" /> Import CSV
                        </button>
                        <button type="button" onClick={() => setShowUploadForm((p) => !p)} className="px-3.5 py-2 bg-[#2BB656] hover:bg-emerald-600 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-xs transition cursor-pointer">
                            {showUploadForm ? <X className="w-4 h-4" /> : <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />}{showUploadForm ? 'Close' : 'Add Invoice'}
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </div>

                {showUploadForm && (
                    <form onSubmit={handleUploadSubmit} noValidate className="mx-5 p-4 rounded-xl bg-emerald-50/60 border border-emerald-200 grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-5 gap-3 text-xs">
                        {photoPreviewUrl && (
                            <div className="md:col-span-5 flex items-center justify-between bg-white p-3 rounded-xl border border-purple-200">
                                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                                    <img src={photoPreviewUrl} alt="Receipt preview" className="w-12 h-12 object-cover rounded-lg border border-slate-200" />
                                    <div>
                                        <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-purple-100 text-purple-800 font-bold text-[10px]">AI Vision OCR Active</span>
                                        <p className="font-bold text-slate-800 mt-0.5">{uploadedFileName}</p>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                                <button type="button" onClick={() => { setPhotoPreviewUrl(null); setUploadedFileName(null); }} className="text-slate-400 hover:text-rose-600 p-1 cursor-pointer"><X className="w-4 h-4" /></button>
                            </div>
                        )}

                        <div>
                            <label className="font-bold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Invoice Number *</label>
                            <input type="text" maxLength={20} placeholder="e.g., SUP-2026-9920" value={invNo} onChange={(e) => { setInvNo(e.target.value); if (formErrors.invNo) setFormErrors({ ...formErrors, invNo: '' }); }} className={`w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-white border ${formErrors.invNo ? 'border-rose-500' : 'border-slate-300'}`} />
                            {formErrors.invNo && <p className="text-[10px] text-rose-600 font-semibold mt-1"><AlertCircle className="w-3 h-3 inline" /> {formErrors.invNo}</p>}
                        </div>
                        <div>
                            <label className="font-bold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Direction *</label>
                            <select value={invType} onChange={(e) => setInvType(e.target.value as 'SUPPLIER_PAYABLE' | 'CUSTOMER_RECEIVABLE')} className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-white border border-slate-300 font-semibold text-slate-900">
                                <option value="SUPPLIER_PAYABLE">Supplier Bill (Payable)</option>
                                <option value="CUSTOMER_RECEIVABLE">Customer Invoice (Receivable)</option>
                            </select>
                        </div>
                        <div>
                            <label className="font-bold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Counterparty Name *</label>
                            <input type="text" maxLength={60} placeholder="e.g., EcoPack Malaysia" value={partyName} onChange={(e) => { setPartyName(e.target.value); if (formErrors.partyName) setFormErrors({ ...formErrors, partyName: '' }); }} className={`w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-white border ${formErrors.partyName ? 'border-rose-500' : 'border-slate-300'}`} />
                        </div>
                        <div>
                            <label className="font-bold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Base Amount ({currency}) *</label>
                            <input type="text" inputMode="decimal" placeholder="1350.00" value={amountInput} onChange={(e) => { setAmountInput(sanitizeDecimal(e.target.value)); if (formErrors.amountInput) setFormErrors({ ...formErrors, amountInput: '' }); }} className={`w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-white border ${formErrors.amountInput ? 'border-rose-500' : 'border-slate-300'}`} />
                        </div>
                        <div>
                            <label className="font-bold text-slate-700 block mb-1">RMCD SST Tax Rate *</label>
                            <select value={newInvSstCode} onChange={(e) => setNewInvSstCode(e.target.value as SstTaxCode)} className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-white border border-slate-300 font-semibold text-slate-900">
                                {SST_TAX_OPTIONS.map((o) => <option key={o.code} value={o.code}>{o.label}</option>)}
                            </select>
                        </div>
                        <div className="md:col-span-4">
                            <label className="font-bold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Invoice Line Items / Description *</label>
                            <input type="text" maxLength={180} placeholder="e.g., 30 boxes biodegradable takeaway packaging" value={rawLineDescription} onChange={(e) => { setRawLineDescription(e.target.value); if (formErrors.rawLineDescription) setFormErrors({ ...formErrors, rawLineDescription: '' }); }} className={`w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-white border ${formErrors.rawLineDescription ? 'border-rose-500' : 'border-slate-300'}`} />
                        </div>
                        <div className="flex items-end gap-2">
                            <button type="button" onClick={() => photoInputRef.current?.click()} className="py-2 px-3 bg-white hover:bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 border border-slate-300 rounded-lg font-bold flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer"><Upload className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Photo</button>
                            <button type="submit" className="flex-1 py-2 px-3 bg-[#2BB656] hover:bg-emerald-600 text-white font-bold rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center gap-1 transition cursor-pointer"><Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Save</button>
                        </div>
                    </form>
                )}

                <div className="px-5 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
                    <div className="flex items-center bg-slate-100 p-1 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 text-xs font-semibold">
                        {[
                            { id: 'ALL' as const, label: `All (${invoices.length})`, active: 'bg-white text-slate-900 shadow-2xs font-bold' },
                            { id: 'SUPPLIER_PAYABLE' as const, label: `Supplier Bills (${invoices.filter((i) => i.type === 'SUPPLIER_PAYABLE').length})`, active: 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-900 font-bold' },
                            { id: 'CUSTOMER_RECEIVABLE' as const, label: `Customer Invoices (${invoices.filter((i) => i.type === 'CUSTOMER_RECEIVABLE').length})`, active: 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-900 font-bold' },
                        ].map((t) => (
                            <button key={t.id} type="button" onClick={() => setDirectionFilter(t.id)} className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition cursor-pointer ${directionFilter === t.id ? t.active : 'text-slate-600'}`}>{t.label}</button>
                        ))}
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
                        <span className="text-slate-500 font-medium">Filter:</span>
                        <button type="button" onClick={() => setStatusFilter(statusFilter === 'NEEDS_REVIEW' ? 'ALL' : 'NEEDS_REVIEW')} className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-lg border font-semibold transition cursor-pointer ${statusFilter === 'NEEDS_REVIEW' ? 'bg-amber-500 text-white border-amber-500' : 'bg-slate-50 text-slate-600 border-slate-200'}`}>
                            Pending Review ({invoices.filter((i) => i.status === 'NEEDS_REVIEW').length})
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                    <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse">
                        <thead>
                        <tr className="border-b border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/70 text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-600 uppercase">
                            <th className="py-3 px-4">Invoice &amp; Source</th>
                            <th className="py-3 px-3">Direction</th>
                            <th className="py-3 px-3">Counterparty</th>
                            <th className="py-3 px-3">AI Categorization</th>
                            <th className="py-3 px-3">SST Rate</th>

                            <th className="py-3 px-3">Payment Status</th>

                        </tr>
                        </thead>
                        <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100 text-xs">
                        {filtered.map((inv) => {

                            const activeCategory = selectedCategories[inv.id] ?? inv.aiCategory;
                            const sst = inferSstForInvoice(inv);

                                <tr key={inv.id} className="hover:bg-slate-50/80 transition">
                                    <td className="py-3 px-4 whitespace-nowrap">
                                        <p className="font-mono font-bold text-slate-900">{inv.invNo}</p>
                                        <span className={`inline-block mt-0.5 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-bold ${source.color}`}>{source.label}</span>
                                    </td>
                                    <td className="py-3 px-3 whitespace-nowrap">
                                        <span className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[11px] font-semibold ${inv.type === 'SUPPLIER_PAYABLE' ? 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-800' : 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-800'}`}>
                                            {inv.type === 'SUPPLIER_PAYABLE' ? 'Supplier Bill' : 'Customer Sales'}
                                        </span>
                                    </td>
                                    <td className="py-3 px-3 font-semibold text-slate-900 max-w-[180px] truncate">
                                        <p className="truncate">{inv.party}</p><p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">{inv.date}</p>
                                    </td>
                                    <td className="py-3 px-3">
                                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                                            {inv.status === 'NEEDS_REVIEW' ? (
                                                <select value={activeCategory} onChange={(e) => setSelectedCategories({ ...selectedCategories, [inv.id]: e.target.value })} className="max-w-[190px] px-2 py-1 rounded-lg bg-purple-50/70 border border-purple-200 font-semibold text-purple-800 text-xs focus:outline-none">
                                                    <option value={inv.aiCategory}>{inv.aiCategory}</option>
                                                    {ACCOUNTING_CATEGORIES.filter((c) => c !== inv.aiCategory).map((cat) => <option key={cat} value={cat}>{cat}</option>)}
                                                </select>
                                            ) : (
                                                <span className="font-semibold text-purple-700">{inv.aiCategory}</span>
                                            )}
                                            <span className="bg-purple-100 text-purple-800 text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded font-bold shrink-0">{inv.aiConfidence}%</span>
                                        </div>
                                    </td>
                                    <td className="py-3 px-3 whitespace-nowrap">
                                        <select
                                            value={sst.code}
                                            onChange={(e) => setSelectedSstCodes({ ...selectedSstCodes, [inv.id]: e.target.value as SstTaxCode })}
                                            className="px-2 py-1 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 border border-slate-200 font-bold text-[11px] text-slate-800"
                                        >
                                            <option value="EXEMPT_0">0% Exempt</option>
                                            <option value="FNB_LOGISTICS_6">6% F&amp;B / Logistics</option>
                                            <option value="SERVICE_8">8% Service Tax</option>
                                            <option value="SALES_10">10% Sales Tax</option>
                                        </select>
                                    </td>
                                    <td className="py-3 px-3 whitespace-nowrap">
                                        <p className="font-extrabold text-slate-900">
                                            {sym} {((inv.amountRM + sst.sstRM) * exchangeRate).toLocaleString(undefined, { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 })}
                                        </p>
                                        <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500">
                                            Base: {sym} {(inv.amountRM * exchangeRate).toFixed(2)} + SST ({sst.ratePct}%): {sym} {(sst.sstRM * exchangeRate).toFixed(2)}
                                        </span>
                                    </td>
                                    <td className="py-3 px-3 whitespace-nowrap">
                                        <button type="button" onClick={() => onToggleInvoicePaid(inv.id)} className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-[11px] font-bold cursor-pointer border transition ${inv.paid ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border-emerald-300 hover:bg-emerald-200' : 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 border-rose-200 hover:bg-rose-100'}`}>
                                            {inv.paid ? 'Paid âœ“' : 'Unpaid (Mark Paid)'}
                                        </button>
                                    </td>
                                    <td className="py-3 px-4 whitespace-nowrap flex items-center gap-2">
                                        {inv.status === 'NEEDS_REVIEW' ? (
                                            <button type="button" onClick={() => onVerifyInvoice(inv.id, activeCategory)} className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-amber-400 hover:bg-amber-500 text-slate-900 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition cursor-pointer">
                                                {canApprove ? 'Confirm' : 'Owner Only'}
                                            </button>
                                        ) : (
                                            <span className="px-2.5 py-1 bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold inline-flex items-center gap-1"><CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-600" /> Verified</span>
                                        )}

                                        {inv.type === 'CUSTOMER_RECEIVABLE' && !inv.paid && (
                                            <button
                                                type="button"
                                                onClick={() => handleSendPaymentReminder(inv, 'EN')}
                                                className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-600 text-white text-[11px] font-bold inline-flex items-center gap-1 shadow-2xs cursor-pointer"
                                            >
                                                Remind Customer
                                            </button>
                                        )}
                                    </td>
                                </tr>
                            );
                        })}
                        </tbody>
                    </table>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};
